import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from pathlib import Path
from IPython.display import display
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
def run_hard4_diagnostic(source_loader):
    SEED = 42
    STEPS = 300
    LR = 1e-3
    LOG_STEPS = {0, 1, 25, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300}
    # Authentic trước, tampered sau.
    indices = [2347, 3403, 7700, 8693]
    expected_names = [
        "Au_cha_30565.jpg",
        "Au_pla_20011.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NNN_S_N_cha10140_cha10140_12198.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_pla20022_pla20022_01922.tif",
    ]
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    dataset = source_loader.dataset
    while hasattr(dataset, "base_dataset"):
        dataset = dataset.base_dataset
    if not hasattr(dataset, "records"):
        raise RuntimeError("Cần v4_train_loader của dataset gốc.")
    # Kiểm tra tên file để tránh dùng sai index sau khi đổi manifest.
    for index, expected in zip(indices, expected_names):
        actual = Path(dataset.records[index]["image_path"]).name
        if actual != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {index} đang trỏ tới {actual}, "
                f"không phải {expected}. Dừng để kiểm tra manifest."
            )
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()]
        if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    history = []
    with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        # Đọc một lần: dữ liệu giữ nguyên ở mọi step.
        samples = [dataset[i] for i in indices]
        images = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        masks = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        del samples
        assert images.shape == (4, 3, 256, 256)
        assert masks.shape == (4, 1, 256, 256)
        assert torch.isfinite(images).all().item()
        assert torch.isfinite(masks).all().item()
        assert 0 <= images.min().item() <= images.max().item() <= 1
        assert ((masks == 0) | (masks == 1)).all().item()
        assert masks[:2].sum().item() == 0
        assert (masks[2:].flatten(1).sum(1) > 0).all().item()
        print("Indices:", indices)
        print(
            "GT foreground từng ảnh (%):",
            (100 * masks.flatten(1).mean(1)).cpu().tolist()
        )

=== PAGE 96 ===
        # Model mới hoàn toàn, không sao chép trọng số V4.
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        model = UNet(
            in_channels=3,
            out_channels=1,
            base_channels=16,
        ).to(device)
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
            model.parameters(),
            lr=LR,
            weight_decay=1e-4,
        )
        pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
        def loss_fn(logits):
            logits = logits.float()
            bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits, masks, pos_weight=pos_weight
            )
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            intersection = (probs * masks).sum()
            denominator = probs.sum() + masks.sum()
            dice_loss = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1.0)
                / (denominator + 1.0)
            )
            return bce + dice_loss, bce, dice_loss
        @torch.no_grad()
        def evaluate(step):
            model.eval()
            logits = model(images)
            loss, bce, dice_loss = loss_fn(logits)
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            preds = probs > 0.5
            truth = masks.bool()
            tp = (preds & truth).sum().item()
            fp = (preds & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn = (~preds & truth).sum().item()
            result = {
                "step": step,
                "loss": loss.item(),
                "bce": bce.item(),
                "soft_dice_loss": dice_loss.item(),
                "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
                "authentic_fp_pct": (
                    100 * preds[:2].float().mean().item()
                ),
            }
            for position, index in [(2, 7700), (3, 8693)]:
                p = preds[position]
                gt = truth[position]
                intersection = (p & gt).sum().item()
                denominator = p.sum().item() + gt.sum().item()
                result[f"dice_{index}"] = (
                    2 * intersection / max(denominator, 1)
                )
                result[f"prob_in_mask_{index}"] = (
                    probs[position][gt].mean().item()
                )
                result[f"prob_background_{index}"] = (
                    probs[position][~gt].mean().item()
                )
            if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in result.values()):
                raise RuntimeError("Evaluation có giá trị không hữu hạn.")
            return result

=== PAGE 97 ===
        print("\nFRESH MODEL | 4 FIXED IMAGES | 300 STEPS")
        for step in range(STEPS + 1):
            if step > 0:
                model.train()
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                logits = model(images)
                loss, _, _ = loss_fn(logits)
                if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite loss tại step {step}")
                loss.backward()
                for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                    if not parameter.requires_grad:
                        continue
                    if parameter.grad is None:
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Thiếu gradient: {name}")
                    if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                optimizer.step()
                del logits, loss
            if step in LOG_STEPS:
                result = evaluate(step)
                history.append(result)
                print(
                    f"Step {step:03d} | Loss {result['loss']:.4f} | "
                    f"Dice 7700 {result['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                    f"Dice 8693 {result['dice_8693']:.4f} | "
                    f"Authentic FP {result['authentic_fp_pct']:.2f}%"
                )
        model.eval()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    # Giữ model và dữ liệu để xem kết quả, không lưu checkpoint.
    return model, images, masks, pd.DataFrame(history)
if "v4_train_loader" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("Cần chạy cell tạo v4_train_loader trước.")
hard4_model, hard4_images, hard4_masks, hard4_history = (

import torch.nn.functional as F

def run_fixed16_300(source_loader):
    SEED = 42
    EPOCHS = 300
    LR = 1e-3
    LOG_EPOCHS = {0, 1, 25, 50, 75, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300}
    # Đúng bộ 16 ảnh đã chạy, không chọn lại theo kết quả.
    indices = [
        2347, 3403, 856, 3032, 2462, 2241, 2008, 1985,
        7700, 8281, 6166, 8448, 6811, 7071, 8693, 4762,
    ]
    expected_names = [
        "Au_cha_30565.jpg",
        "Au_pla_20011.jpg",
        "Au_arc_30094.jpg",
        "Au_nat_30260.jpg",
        "Au_ind_00025.jpg",
        "Au_cha_30376.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10175.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10150.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NNN_S_N_cha10140_cha10140_12198.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRD_S_N_ind00028_ind00028_00895.tif",
        "Tp_D_NRN_S_N_pla00008_pla00009_10936.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_arc00051_arc00051_11208.jpg",
        "Tp_S_CRN_S_B_arc00095_arc00095_01035.tif",
        "Tp_S_NND_S_N_sec20028_sec20028_01618.tif",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_pla20022_pla20022_01922.tif",
        "Tp_D_CRN_M_O_art10113_art10112_11560.jpg",
    ]
    dataset = source_loader.dataset
    while hasattr(dataset, "base_dataset"):
        dataset = dataset.base_dataset
    if not hasattr(dataset, "records"):
        raise RuntimeError("Cần v4_train_loader của dataset gốc.")
    for index, expected in zip(indices, expected_names):
        actual = Path(dataset.records[index]["image_path"]).name
        if actual != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {index} đang trỏ tới {actual}. "
                "Manifest đã thay đổi; dừng để kiểm tra."
            )
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    print("Indices:", indices)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()]
        if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    history = []
    per_image_history = []
    with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        # Đọc một lần, cố định input trong toàn bộ thí nghiệm.
        samples = [dataset[i] for i in indices]
        images = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        masks = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )

=== PAGE 100 ===
        del samples
        assert images.shape == (16, 3, 256, 256)
        assert masks.shape == (16, 1, 256, 256)
        assert torch.isfinite(images).all().item()
        assert torch.isfinite(masks).all().item()
        assert 0 <= images.min().item() <= images.max().item() <= 1
        assert ((masks == 0) | (masks == 1)).all().item()
        positive = masks.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
        assert not positive[:8].any().item()
        assert positive[8:].all().item()
        print(f"GT foreground: {100 * masks.mean().item():.4f}%")
        print("Kỳ vọng gần 1.8255% như lần trước.")
        # Model mới; không dùng trọng số hard4_model hoặc V4.
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        model = UNet(
            in_channels=3,
            out_channels=1,
            base_channels=16,
        ).to(device)
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
            model.parameters(),
            lr=LR,
            weight_decay=1e-4,
        )
        pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
        def loss_fn(logits, targets):
            logits = logits.float()
            targets = targets.float()
            bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
            )
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
            denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
            soft_dice = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1.0)
                / (denominator + 1.0)
            )
            return bce + soft_dice, bce, soft_dice
        @torch.no_grad()
        def evaluate(epoch):
            model.eval()
            logits = model(images)
            loss, bce, soft_dice = loss_fn(logits, masks)
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            preds = probs > 0.5
            truth = masks.bool()
            tp = (preds & truth).sum().item()
            fp = (preds & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn = (~preds & truth).sum().item()
            intersections = (
                preds & truth
            ).flatten(1).sum(1).float()
            denominators = (
                preds.flatten(1).sum(1)
                + truth.flatten(1).sum(1)
            ).float()
            dice_per_image = (
                (2 * intersections + 1e-6)
                / (denominators + 1e-6)
            )

=== PAGE 101 ===
            metrics = {
                "epoch": epoch,
                "optimizer_steps": epoch * 2,
                "eval_loss": loss.item(),
                "bce": bce.item(),
                "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice.item(),
                "micro_dice_05": (
                    2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1)
                ),
                "tampered_dice_05": (
                    dice_per_image[positive].mean().item()
                ),
                "authentic_fp_pct": (
                    100 * preds[:8].float().mean().item()
                ),
            }
            for position, index in enumerate(indices):
                if position < 8:
                    continue
                gt = truth[position]
                record = {
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "dataset_index": index,
                    "filename": expected_names[position],
                    "dice_05": dice_per_image[position].item(),
                    "gt_foreground_pct": (
                        100 * masks[position].mean().item()
                    ),
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * preds[position].float().mean().item()
                    ),
                    "prob_in_mask": probs[position][gt].mean().item(),
                    "prob_background": (
                        probs[position][~gt].mean().item()
                    ),
                }
                per_image_history.append(record)
                if index in (7700, 8693):
                    metrics[f"dice_{index}"] = record["dice_05"]
            if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in metrics.values()):
                raise RuntimeError("Evaluation không hữu hạn.")
            return metrics
        # Giữ đúng hai batch của thí nghiệm 16 ảnh trước.
        batches = [
            [0, 1, 2, 3, 8, 9, 10, 11],
            [4, 5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15],
        ]
        for epoch in range(EPOCHS + 1):
            if epoch > 0:
                model.train()
                for batch_indices in batches:
                    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    logits = model(images[batch_indices])
                    loss, _, _ = loss_fn(
                        logits, masks[batch_indices]
                    )
                    if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                        raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite loss")
                    loss.backward()
                    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                        if not parameter.requires_grad:
                            continue
                        if parameter.grad is None:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Thiếu gradient: {name}")
                        if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():

=== PAGE 102 ===
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                    optimizer.step()
                    del logits, loss
            if epoch in LOG_EPOCHS:
                metrics = evaluate(epoch)
                history.append(metrics)
                print(
                    f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | "
                    f"Steps {epoch * 2:03d} | "
                    f"Loss {metrics['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
                    f"Micro Dice {metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                    f"Dice 7700 {metrics['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                    f"Dice 8693 {metrics['dice_8693']:.4f}"
                )
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        model.eval()
        del optimizer
    return (
        model,
        images,
        masks,
        pd.DataFrame(history),
        pd.DataFrame(per_image_history),
        indices,
    )
if "v4_train_loader" not in globals():

import copy
import gc
import numpy as np

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
def compare_learning_rate_decay(images_input, masks_input, indices):
    SEED = 42
    SPLIT_EPOCH = 150
    FINAL_EPOCH = 300
    expected_indices = [
        2347, 3403, 856, 3032, 2462, 2241, 2008, 1985,
        7700, 8281, 6166, 8448, 6811, 7071, 8693, 4762,
    ]
    if list(indices) != expected_indices:

=== PAGE 105 ===
        raise RuntimeError("Bộ ảnh không trùng với thí nghiệm 16 ảnh trước.")
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    # Clone để không thay đổi các tensor của thí nghiệm trước.
    images = images_input.detach().clone().to(
        device, dtype=torch.float32
    )
    masks = masks_input.detach().clone().to(
        device, dtype=torch.float32
    )
    assert images.shape == (16, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks.shape == (16, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks).all().item()
    assert ((masks == 0) | (masks == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:8].any().item()
    assert positive[8:].all().item()
    print("Device:", device)
    print(f"GT foreground: {100 * masks.mean().item():.4f}%")
    batches = [
        [0, 1, 2, 3, 8, 9, 10, 11],
        [4, 5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15],
    ]
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_fn(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * intersection + 1.0)
            / (denominator + 1.0)
        )
        return bce + dice_loss
    def train_epoch(model, optimizer):
        model.train()
        for batch_indices in batches:
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            logits = model(images[batch_indices])
            loss = loss_fn(logits, masks[batch_indices])
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite loss")
            loss.backward()
            for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                if not parameter.requires_grad:
                    continue
                if parameter.grad is None:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Thiếu gradient: {name}")
                if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
            optimizer.step()
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(model, epoch, phase, lr):
        model.eval()

=== PAGE 106 ===
        logits = model(images)
        loss = loss_fn(logits, masks)
        pred = logits.sigmoid() > 0.5
        truth = masks.bool()
        tp = (pred & truth).sum().item()
        fp = (pred & ~truth).sum().item()
        fn = (~pred & truth).sum().item()
        intersection = (pred & truth).flatten(1).sum(1).float()
        denominator = (
            pred.flatten(1).sum(1)
            + truth.flatten(1).sum(1)
        ).float()
        per_image = (
            (2 * intersection + 1e-6)
            / (denominator + 1e-6)
        )
        row = {
            "phase": phase,
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 2,
            "lr": lr,
            "eval_loss": loss.item(),
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_05": per_image[positive].mean().item(),
            "authentic_fp_pct": (
                100 * pred[:8].float().mean().item()
            ),
        }
        # Lưu Dice của toàn bộ 8 ảnh tampered.
        for position in range(8, 16):
            row[f"dice_{indices[position]}"] = per_image[position].item()
        numeric_values = [
            value for value in row.values()
            if isinstance(value, (int, float))
        ]
        if not all(np.isfinite(value) for value in numeric_values):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics")
        print(
            f"{phase:6s} | Epoch {epoch:03d} | LR {lr:.0e} | "
            f"Loss {row['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
            f"Micro {row['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
            f"7700 {row['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
            f"8693 {row['dice_8693']:.4f}"
        )
        return row
    def cpu_snapshot(value):
        """Sao chép state sang CPU để giữ riêng từng nhánh."""
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_snapshot(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()]
        if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    # Lưu và khôi phục thiết lập của notebook sau thí nghiệm.
    old_deterministic = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn_only = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic

=== PAGE 107 ===
    history = []
    final_states = {}
    try:
        # Giảm tính không xác định; không bảo đảm giống nhau giữa
        # các phiên bản PyTorch hoặc phần cứng khác nhau.
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
            torch.manual_seed(SEED)
            model = UNet(
                in_channels=3,
                out_channels=1,
                base_channels=16,
            ).to(device)
            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                model.parameters(),
                lr=1e-3,
                weight_decay=1e-4,
            )
            print("\n=== CHUNG: 150 LƯỢT ĐẦU ===")
            history.append(evaluate(model, 0, "SHARED", 1e-3))
            for epoch in range(1, SPLIT_EPOCH + 1):
                train_epoch(model, optimizer)
                if epoch % 10 == 0:
                    history.append(
                        evaluate(model, epoch, "SHARED", 1e-3)
                    )
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            # Cả hai nhánh bắt đầu từ cùng weights và AdamW state.
            split_model_state = cpu_snapshot(model.state_dict())
            split_optimizer_state = cpu_snapshot(optimizer.state_dict())
            split_cpu_rng = torch.get_rng_state().clone()
            split_cuda_rng = (
                torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device).clone()
                if device.type == "cuda" else None
            )
            del model, optimizer
            gc.collect()
            if device.type == "cuda":
                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
            for branch, learning_rate in [
                ("A", 1e-3),
                ("B", 3e-4),
            ]:
                print(
                    f"\n=== NHÁNH {branch}: "
                    f"LR={learning_rate} TỪ LƯỢT 151 ==="
                )
                model = UNet(
                    in_channels=3,
                    out_channels=1,
                    base_channels=16,
                ).to(device)
                model.load_state_dict(split_model_state)
                optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                    model.parameters(),
                    lr=1e-3,
                    weight_decay=1e-4,
                )
                optimizer.load_state_dict(
                    copy.deepcopy(split_optimizer_state)
                )

=== PAGE 108 ===
                # Phải đổi LR sau khi load optimizer state.
                for group in optimizer.param_groups:
                    group["lr"] = learning_rate
                torch.set_rng_state(split_cpu_rng)
                if device.type == "cuda":
                    torch.cuda.set_rng_state(split_cuda_rng, device)
                # Hai dòng epoch 150 phải có metric giống nhau.
                history.append(
                    evaluate(
                        model, SPLIT_EPOCH, branch, learning_rate
                    )
                )
                for epoch in range(
                    SPLIT_EPOCH + 1, FINAL_EPOCH + 1
                ):
                    train_epoch(model, optimizer)
                    if epoch % 10 == 0:
                        history.append(
                            evaluate(
                                model, epoch, branch, learning_rate
                            )
                        )
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                final_states[branch] = cpu_snapshot(model.state_dict())
                del model, optimizer
                gc.collect()
                if device.type == "cuda":
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    finally:
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_deterministic, warn_only=old_warn_only
        )
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_deterministic
    return pd.DataFrame(history), final_states

# First run: leave None.
# To resume: paste the exact last.pt path printed by this cell.

# /content is temporary storage.
# Change to a mounted Google Drive directory if persistence is needed.
OUTPUT_ROOT = Path("/content/diagnostics")
def run_diagnostic32(source_loader, total_epochs, output_root, resume_path=None):
    SEED = 42
    LR = 1e-3
    WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4
    LOG_EVERY = 10
    old_indices = [
        2347, 3403, 856, 3032, 2462, 2241, 2008, 1985,
        7700, 8281, 6166, 8448, 6811, 7071, 8693, 4762,
    ]
    old_names = [
        "Au_cha_30565.jpg",
        "Au_pla_20011.jpg",
        "Au_arc_30094.jpg",
        "Au_nat_30260.jpg",
        "Au_ind_00025.jpg",
        "Au_cha_30376.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10175.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10150.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NNN_S_N_cha10140_cha10140_12198.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRD_S_N_ind00028_ind00028_00895.tif",
        "Tp_D_NRN_S_N_pla00008_pla00009_10936.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_arc00051_arc00051_11208.jpg",
        "Tp_S_CRN_S_B_arc00095_arc00095_01035.tif",
        "Tp_S_NND_S_N_sec20028_sec20028_01618.tif",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_pla20022_pla20022_01922.tif",
        "Tp_D_CRN_M_O_art10113_art10112_11560.jpg",
    ]
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    source = source_loader.dataset
    if not isinstance(source, Subset) or len(source) != 256:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "train_loader phải chứa Subset TRAIN 256 ảnh của B/C."
        )
    def resolve_sample(dataset, index):
        while True:
            if isinstance(dataset, Subset):
                index = int(dataset.indices[index])
                dataset = dataset.dataset
            elif hasattr(dataset, "base_dataset"):
                dataset = dataset.base_dataset
            else:
                return dataset, index
    references = [resolve_sample(source, i) for i in range(256)]
    base = references[0][0]
    if not hasattr(base, "records"):
        raise RuntimeError("Dataset gốc không có .records.")
    if any(dataset is not base for dataset, _ in references):
        raise RuntimeError("Subset phải dùng cùng một dataset gốc.")

=== PAGE 112 ===
    source_ids = [index for _, index in references]
    if len(set(source_ids)) != 256:
        raise RuntimeError("Subset TRAIN chứa index trùng nhau.")
    source_ids_set = set(source_ids)
    if not set(old_indices).issubset(source_ids_set):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Subset 256 hiện tại không chứa đủ 16 ảnh cũ. "
            "Không tiếp tục với một subset khác."
        )
    for index, expected in zip(old_indices, old_names):
        actual = Path(base.records[index]["image_path"]).name
        if actual != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {index}: {actual} khác {expected}."
            )
    def label_of(index):
        label = str(base.records[index]["label"]).strip().lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(label, label)
    # Select additions only from the remaining 256-image TRAIN subset.
    remaining = sorted(source_ids_set - set(old_indices))
    auth_candidates = [
        i for i in remaining if label_of(i) == "authentic"
    ]
    tamp_candidates = [
        i for i in remaining if label_of(i) == "tampered"
    ]
    if min(len(auth_candidates), len(tamp_candidates)) < 8:
        raise RuntimeError("Không đủ ảnh để thêm 8 authentic + 8 tampered.")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    added_auth = rng.choice(
        auth_candidates, 8, replace=False
    ).tolist()
    added_tamp = rng.choice(
        tamp_candidates, 8, replace=False
    ).tolist()
    # Positions 0–15 are authentic; 16–31 are tampered.
    # First two batches preserve the previous 16-image grouping.
    indices = (
        old_indices[:8] + added_auth
        + old_indices[8:] + added_tamp
    )
    assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == 32
    batches = [
        list(range(start, start + 4))
        + list(range(16 + start, 20 + start))
        for start in (0, 4, 8, 12)
    ]
    samples = [base[i] for i in indices]
    images_cpu = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).float()
    masks_cpu = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).float()
    del samples
    assert images_cpu.shape == (32, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks_cpu.shape == (32, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images_cpu).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks_cpu).all().item()
    assert 0 <= images_cpu.min().item() <= images_cpu.max().item() <= 1
    assert ((masks_cpu == 0) | (masks_cpu == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks_cpu.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:16].any().item()
    assert positive[16:].all().item()
    # Fingerprint the actual preprocessed tensors for safe resume.
    import hashlib
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(images_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    digest.update(masks_cpu.numpy().tobytes())

=== PAGE 113 ===
    tensor_hash = digest.hexdigest()
    sample_rows = []
    for position, index in enumerate(indices):
        sample_rows.append({
            "position": position,
            "dataset_index": index,
            "filename": Path(base.records[index]["image_path"]).name,
            "label": label_of(index),
            "selection": "previous16" if index in old_indices else "added16",
            "gt_foreground_pct": (
                100 * masks_cpu[position].mean().item()
            ),
        })
    print("Images: 32 | Batches per epoch: 4")
    print("Maximum optimizer steps:", total_epochs * 4)
    print(f"GT foreground: {100 * masks_cpu.mean().item():.4f}%")
    display(pd.DataFrame(sample_rows))
    config = {
        "seed": SEED,
        "lr": LR,
        "weight_decay": WEIGHT_DECAY,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_global_dice_smooth1",
        "indices": indices,
        "batches": batches,
        "tensor_sha256": tensor_hash,
    }
    if resume_path is None:
        run_dir = Path(output_root) / (
            "fixed32_" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        saved = None
    else:
        resume_path = Path(resume_path)
        saved = torch.load(
            resume_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
        )
        if saved.get("kind") != "fixed32_diagnostic_v1":
            raise RuntimeError("Checkpoint không phải của cell chẩn đoán này.")
        if saved["config"] != config:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Dữ liệu hoặc cấu hình khác checkpoint. Không resume."
            )
        if saved["epoch"] > total_epochs:
            raise RuntimeError("TOTAL_EPOCHS nhỏ hơn epoch đã lưu.")
        run_dir = resume_path.parent
    pd.DataFrame(sample_rows).to_csv(
        run_dir / "selected_samples.csv", index=False
    )
    images = images_cpu.to(device)
    masks = masks_cpu.to(device)
    del images_cpu, masks_cpu
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        # Return sums so evaluation loss can be computed globally
        # across all 32 images, independently of evaluation batch size.
        bce_sum = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight, reduction="sum"
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        return bce_sum, intersection, denominator

=== PAGE 114 ===
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(model, epoch):
        model.eval()
        total_bce = total_intersection = total_denominator = 0.0
        pixel_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_pred = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        # Evaluate in chunks to reduce GPU memory use.
        for start in range(0, 32, 8):
            stop = start + 8
            targets = masks[start:stop]
            logits = model(images[start:stop])
            bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                logits, targets
            )
            total_bce += bce_sum.item()
            total_intersection += intersection.item()
            total_denominator += denominator.item()
            pixel_count += targets.numel()
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            pred = probs > 0.5
            truth = targets.bool()
            tp += (pred & truth).sum().item()
            fp += (pred & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn += (~pred & truth).sum().item()
            for local in range(stop - start):
                position = start + local
                gt = truth[local]
                prediction = pred[local]
                is_tampered = position >= 16
                if is_tampered:
                    intersection_i = (prediction & gt).sum().item()
                    denominator_i = (
                        prediction.sum().item() + gt.sum().item()
                    )
                    dice = 2 * intersection_i / denominator_i
                    prob_inside = probs[local][gt].mean().item()
                else:
                    dice = None
                    prob_inside = None
                    auth_pred += prediction.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += prediction.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    **sample_rows[position],
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * prediction.float().mean().item()
                    ),
                    "prob_in_mask": prob_inside,
                })
        bce = total_bce / pixel_count
        soft_dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * total_intersection + 1)
            / (total_denominator + 1)
        )
        tampered = [r for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        dice_lookup = {
            r["dataset_index"]: r["dice_05"] for r in tampered
        }
        result = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 4,
            "eval_loss": bce + soft_dice_loss,
            "bce": bce,
            "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice_loss,

=== PAGE 115 ===
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_05": float(
                np.mean([r["dice_05"] for r in tampered])
            ),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(
                r["dice_05"] < 0.1 for r in tampered
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_pred / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "dice_7700": dice_lookup[7700],
            "dice_8693": dice_lookup[8693],
        }
        if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in result.values()):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics")
        return result, details
    def cpu_snapshot(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_snapshot(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def atomic_save(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    old_deterministic = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn_only = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    history = []
    per_image_history = []
    best_macro = -1.0
    start_epoch = 0
    print("\nOutput directory:", run_dir)
    print("Fresh training" if saved is None else "Resuming training")
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
            torch.manual_seed(SEED)
            model = UNet(
                in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
            ).to(device)
            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY
            )
            if saved is not None:
                model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
                optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
                history = saved["history"]
                per_image_history = saved["per_image_history"]
                best_macro = saved["best_macro"]
                start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
                torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
                if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                    torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
                del saved

=== PAGE 116 ===
            for epoch in range(start_epoch, total_epochs + 1):
                if epoch > 0:
                    model.train()
                    for batch_indices in batches:
                        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                        targets = masks[batch_indices]
                        logits = model(images[batch_indices])
                        bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                            logits, targets
                        )
                        loss = (
                            bce_sum / targets.numel()
                            + 1
                            - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
                        )
                        if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss")
                        loss.backward()
                        for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                            if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                continue
                            if parameter.grad is None:
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                            if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                        optimizer.step()
                        del logits, loss, bce_sum, intersection, denominator
                if epoch % LOG_EVERY == 0 or epoch == total_epochs:
                    metrics, details = evaluate(model, epoch)
                    history.append(metrics)
                    per_image_history.extend(details)
                    print(
                        f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Steps {epoch * 4:04d} | "
                        f"Loss {metrics['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
                        f"Micro {metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                        f"Mean Tp {metrics['tampered_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                        f"Tp Dice<0.1: {metrics['tampered_dice_below_01']}/16 | "
                        f"7700 {metrics['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                        f"8693 {metrics['dice_8693']:.4f}"
                    )
                    model_state = cpu_snapshot(model.state_dict())
                    # Diagnostic selection based on TRAIN metrics only.
                    # This is NOT a model selected for final research evaluation.
                    if metrics["tampered_dice_05"] > best_macro:
                        best_macro = metrics["tampered_dice_05"]
                        atomic_save({
                            "kind": "fixed32_diagnostic_best_weights",
                            "epoch": epoch,
                            "config": config,
                            "metrics": metrics,
                            "model_state": model_state,
                        }, run_dir / "best_train_macro.pt")
                    atomic_save({
                        "kind": "fixed32_diagnostic_v1",
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "config": config,
                        "model_state": model_state,
                        "optimizer_state": cpu_snapshot(optimizer.state_dict()),
                        "history": history,
                        "per_image_history": per_image_history,
                        "best_macro": best_macro,
                        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                        "cuda_rng": (
                            torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),

=== PAGE 117 ===
                    }, run_dir / "last.pt")
                    pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    pd.DataFrame(per_image_history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "per_image_history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    del model_state
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            model.eval()
    finally:
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_deterministic, warn_only=old_warn_only
        )
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_deterministic
    return (
        model,
        pd.DataFrame(history),
        pd.DataFrame(per_image_history),
        run_dir,
    )
if "train_loader" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("Cần train_loader của subset TRAIN 256 ảnh B/C.")
# Move only previous diagnostic models off the GPU.
for name in ("hard4_model", "fixed16_model", "diag32_model"):

    # First two batches preserve the previous 16-image grouping.

        old_indices[:8] + added_auth
        + old_indices[8:] + added_tamp
    )
    assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == 32

        list(range(start, start + 4))
        + list(range(16 + start, 20 + start))
        for start in (0, 4, 8, 12)
    ]
    samples = [base[i] for i in indices]
    images_cpu = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).float()
    masks_cpu = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).float()
    del samples
    assert images_cpu.shape == (32, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks_cpu.shape == (32, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images_cpu).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks_cpu).all().item()
    assert 0 <= images_cpu.min().item() <= images_cpu.max().item() <= 1
    assert ((masks_cpu == 0) | (masks_cpu == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks_cpu.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:16].any().item()
    assert positive[16:].all().item()
    # Fingerprint the actual preprocessed tensors for safe resume.
    import hashlib
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(images_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    digest.update(masks_cpu.numpy().tobytes())

    tensor_hash = digest.hexdigest()

    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        # Return sums so evaluation loss can be computed globally
        # across all 32 images, independently of evaluation batch size.
        bce_sum = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight, reduction="sum"
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        return bce_sum, intersection, denominator

=== PAGE 123 ===
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(model, epoch):
        model.eval()
        total_bce = total_intersection = total_denominator = 0.0
        pixel_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_pred = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        # Evaluate in chunks to reduce GPU memory use.
        for start in range(0, 32, 8):
            stop = start + 8
            targets = masks[start:stop]
            logits = model(images[start:stop])
            bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                logits, targets
            )
            total_bce += bce_sum.item()
            total_intersection += intersection.item()
            total_denominator += denominator.item()
            pixel_count += targets.numel()
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            pred = probs > 0.5
            truth = targets.bool()
            tp += (pred & truth).sum().item()
            fp += (pred & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn += (~pred & truth).sum().item()
            for local in range(stop - start):
                position = start + local
                gt = truth[local]
                prediction = pred[local]
                is_tampered = position >= 16
                if is_tampered:
                    intersection_i = (prediction & gt).sum().item()
                    denominator_i = (
                        prediction.sum().item() + gt.sum().item()
                    )
                    dice = 2 * intersection_i / denominator_i
                    prob_inside = probs[local][gt].mean().item()
                else:
                    dice = None
                    prob_inside = None
                    auth_pred += prediction.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += prediction.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    **sample_rows[position],
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * prediction.float().mean().item()
                    ),
                    "prob_in_mask": prob_inside,
                })
        bce = total_bce / pixel_count
        soft_dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * total_intersection + 1)
            / (total_denominator + 1)
        )
        tampered = [r for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        dice_lookup = {
            r["dataset_index"]: r["dice_05"] for r in tampered
        }
        result = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 4,
            "eval_loss": bce + soft_dice_loss,
            "bce": bce,
            "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice_loss,

=== PAGE 124 ===
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_05": float(
                np.mean([r["dice_05"] for r in tampered])
            ),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(
                r["dice_05"] < 0.1 for r in tampered
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_pred / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "dice_7700": dice_lookup[7700],
            "dice_8693": dice_lookup[8693],
        }
        if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in result.values()):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics")
        return result, details
    def cpu_snapshot(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_snapshot(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def atomic_save(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    old_deterministic = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn_only = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    history = []
    per_image_history = []
    best_macro = -1.0
    start_epoch = 0
    print("\nOutput directory:", run_dir)
    print("Fresh training" if saved is None else "Resuming training")
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
            torch.manual_seed(SEED)
            model = UNet(
                in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16

            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY

            if saved is not None:

                optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
                history = saved["history"]
                per_image_history = saved["per_image_history"]

                start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
                torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
                if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                    torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
                del saved

=== PAGE 125 ===
            for epoch in range(start_epoch, total_epochs + 1):

                    model.train()
                    for batch_indices in batches:

                        targets = masks[batch_indices]
                        logits = model(images[batch_indices])
                        bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                            logits, targets
                        )

                            bce_sum / targets.numel()
                            + 1

                        optimizer.step()

                        "kind": "fixed32_diagnostic_v1",
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "config": config,
                        "model_state": model_state,
                        "optimizer_state": cpu_snapshot(optimizer.state_dict()),

                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),

=== PAGE 129 ===
for root in search_roots:
    if not root.exists():
        continue
    for current, directories, filenames in os.walk(root):
        directories[:] = [
            name for name in directories if name not in skip_dirs
        ]
        for filename in filenames:
            if filename not in wanted:
                continue
            path = Path(current) / filename
            identity = str(path.resolve())
            if identity not in seen:
                seen.add(identity)
                found.append(path)
print("=== FILES FOUND ===")
for path in found:
    print(path)
print("\n=== CHECKPOINT IDENTIFICATION ===")

        continue

            path,
            map_location="cpu",
            weights_only=True,
        )

            print(path, "→ Không phải checkpoint dictionary")
            continue
        kind = checkpoint.get("kind", "không có trường kind")
        epoch = checkpoint.get("epoch", "không rõ")
        print(f"\nFile: {path}")
        print("Kind:", kind)
        print("Epoch:", epoch)
        if kind == "fixed32_diagnostic_v1":
            diagnostic_checkpoints.append(path)
            print("ĐÚNG loại checkpoint chẩn đoán 32 ảnh.")
            print(
                "Số ảnh trong cấu hình:",
                len(checkpoint.get("config", {}).get("indices", [])),
            )
        del checkpoint
    except Exception as error:
        print(f"\nKhông đọc được {path}: {error}")

assert "train_dataset" in globals(), "Chạy startup trước."

assert len(train_dataset) == 8765
assert len(val_dataset) == 1768
SEED = 42
rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
# Giữ đúng thuật toán và thứ tự gọi RNG trong notebook trước.
def select_balanced(dataset, n_auth, n_tamp):
    auth = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(dataset.records)
        if str(r["label"]).lower() in ("authentic", "au")
    ]
    tamp = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(dataset.records)
        if str(r["label"]).lower() in ("tampered", "tp")
    ]
    assert len(auth) >= n_auth
    assert len(tamp) >= n_tamp
    selected = (
        rng.choice(auth, n_auth, replace=False).tolist()
        + rng.choice(tamp, n_tamp, replace=False).tolist()
    )
    rng.shuffle(selected)
    return selected
train_indices = select_balanced(train_dataset, 128, 128)
val_indices = select_balanced(val_dataset, 64, 64)

selection_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
def save_selection(dataset, indices, filename):
    rows = []
    for index in indices:
        record = dataset.records[index]
        rows.append({
            "dataset_index": index,
            "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
            "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
            "mask_path": (
                str(record["mask_path"])
                if record["mask_path"] else ""
            ),
            "label": record["label"],
            "source_group": record["source_group"],
        })
    pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(
        selection_dir / filename, index=False
    )
save_selection(train_dataset, train_indices, "train256.csv")
save_selection(val_dataset, val_indices, "validation128.csv")
save_selection(train_dataset, expected32, "diagnostic32.csv")

        "status": "Reconstructed using the earlier selection algorithm",
        "validation_matches_previous_run": "Not independently verified",
    }, indent=2),
    encoding="utf-8",
)

def run_fixed32_again(
    dataset, selection_dir, output_root, total_epochs=300, resume_path=None
):
    seed = 42
    lr = 1e-3
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    selection_path = selection_dir / "diagnostic32.csv"
    if not selection_path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(selection_path)
    selection = pd.read_csv(selection_path, keep_default_na=False)
    indices = selection["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
    assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == 32
    # Check file identities before reading the samples.
    for row in selection.itertuples(index=False):
        record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
        actual = Path(record["image_path"]).name
        if actual != row.filename:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {row.dataset_index}: {actual} != {row.filename}"
            )
        if str(record["label"]) != str(row.label):
            raise RuntimeError("Label changed since selection was saved.")
    # Load once: no changing input or random augmentation during training.
    samples = [dataset[index] for index in indices]
    images_cpu = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).float()
    masks_cpu = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).float()
    del samples
    assert images_cpu.shape == (32, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks_cpu.shape == (32, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images_cpu).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks_cpu).all().item()
    assert 0 <= images_cpu.min().item() <= images_cpu.max().item() <= 1
    assert ((masks_cpu == 0) | (masks_cpu == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks_cpu.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:16].any().item()
    assert positive[16:].all().item()
    # Bind resume to the actual image/mask tensors.
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(images_cpu.numpy().tobytes())

    digest.update(masks_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    config = {
        "seed": seed,
        "lr": lr,
        "weight_decay": 1e-4,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_global_dice_smooth1",
        "indices": indices,
        "tensor_sha256": digest.hexdigest(),

    if resume_path is None:

            "fixed32_rerun_"

            resume_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True

            raise ValueError("Dữ liệu hoặc cấu hình đã thay đổi.")

    # Keep the reconstructed validation identities beside the checkpoints.

    print("Images: 32 | Batches per epoch: 4")

    images = images_cpu.to(device)
    masks = masks_cpu.to(device)
    del images_cpu, masks_cpu
    # Same four balanced batches as the previous 32-image experiment.
    batches = [
        list(range(start, start + 4))
        + list(range(16 + start, 20 + start))
        for start in (0, 4, 8, 12)
    ]
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        bce_sum = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits,
            targets,
            pos_weight=pos_weight,
            reduction="sum",

        )
        probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probabilities * targets).sum()
        denominator = probabilities.sum() + targets.sum()
        return bce_sum, intersection, denominator
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(model, epoch):
        model.eval()
        bce_total = intersection_total = denominator_total = 0.0
        pixel_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_predictions = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        for start in range(0, 32, 8):
            targets = masks[start:start + 8]
            logits = model(images[start:start + 8])
            if not torch.isfinite(logits).all().item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation logits.")
            bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(logits, targets)
            bce_total += bce_sum.item()
            intersection_total += intersection.item()
            denominator_total += denominator.item()
            pixel_count += targets.numel()
            probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
            predictions = probabilities > 0.5
            truth = targets.bool()
            tp += (predictions & truth).sum().item()
            fp += (predictions & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn += (~predictions & truth).sum().item()
            for local in range(8):
                position = start + local
                pred = predictions[local]
                gt = truth[local]
                tampered = position >= 16
                if tampered:
                    numerator = 2 * (pred & gt).sum().item()
                    denominator_i = pred.sum().item() + gt.sum().item()
                    dice = numerator / denominator_i
                    probability_in_mask = probabilities[local][gt].mean().item()
                else:
                    dice = None
                    probability_in_mask = None
                    auth_predictions += pred.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += pred.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "dataset_index": indices[position],
                    "filename": selection.iloc[position]["filename"],
                    "label": "tampered" if tampered else "authentic",
                    "gt_foreground_pct": (
                        100 * targets[local].mean().item()
                    ),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * pred.float().mean().item()
                    ),
                    "prob_in_mask": probability_in_mask,
                })
        tampered_rows = [r for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        lookup = {r["dataset_index"]: r["dice_05"] for r in tampered_rows}
        bce = bce_total / pixel_count
        soft_dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * intersection_total + 1)
            / (denominator_total + 1)

        )
        metrics = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 4,
            "eval_loss": bce + soft_dice_loss,
            "bce": bce,
            "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice_loss,
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_05": float(
                np.mean([r["dice_05"] for r in tampered_rows])

                r["dice_05"] < 0.1 for r in tampered_rows

        if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in metrics.values()):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics.")
        return metrics, details
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_checkpoint(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    old_deterministic = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn_only = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []

                        optimizer.step()

                if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == total_epochs:

                        "config": config,
                        "model_state": state,

                        "best_macro": best_macro,

                        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                        "cuda_rng": (
                            torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),
                    }, run_dir / "last.pt")
                    pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "history.csv", index=False

THRESHOLD = 0.5

def run_train256():
    if not Path("/content/drive/MyDrive").is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError("Google Drive chưa được mount.")
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy cell startup để tạo train_dataset/val_dataset.")

=== PAGE 144 ===
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    else:
        print("CPU mode: training sẽ chậm.")
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Restore exact saved selections.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    def restore_selection(dataset, filename, count):
        table = pd.read_csv(
            SELECTION_DIR / filename, keep_default_na=False
        )
        ids = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        if len(ids) != count or len(set(ids)) != count:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid selection: {filename}")
        records = []
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Selection mismatch: {filename}, index {row.dataset_index}"
                )
            records.append(record)
        return Subset(dataset, ids), records, table
    train_subset, train_records, train_table = restore_selection(
        train_dataset, "train256.csv", 256
    )
    val_subset, val_records, val_table = restore_selection(
        val_dataset, "validation128.csv", 128
    )
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Check full TRAIN256/VAL128 overlap.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    def resolved_values(records, field):
        values = set()
        for record in records:
            if field not in record:
                raise ValueError(f"Records thiếu {field}")
            value = record[field]
            if value is None or str(value) == "":
                continue
            values.add(
                str(Path(value).resolve())
                if field.endswith("_path") else str(value)
            )
        return values
    for field in ("image_path", "mask_path", "source_group", "image_hash"):
        overlap = (
            resolved_values(train_records, field)
            & resolved_values(val_records, field)
        )
        if overlap:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Cross-split overlap: {field}: {sorted(overlap)[:5]}. "
                "Dừng để sửa split, không bỏ qua kiểm tra."
            )
    print("TRAIN256 / VAL128 overlap checks: PASSED")
    print("Kiểm tra này không phát hiện mọi quan hệ donor/near-duplicate chưa biết.")
    def normalized_label(record):
        value = str(record["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    train_auth = [

=== PAGE 145 ===
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records)
        if normalized_label(r) == "authentic"
    ]
    train_tamp = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records)
        if normalized_label(r) == "tampered"
    ]
    val_auth = sum(normalized_label(r) == "authentic" for r in val_records)
    val_tamp = sum(normalized_label(r) == "tampered" for r in val_records)
    assert len(train_auth) == len(train_tamp) == 128
    assert val_auth == val_tamp == 64
    # Inspect every preprocessed sample and bind resume to actual tensors.
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        positive_pixels = total_pixels = 0
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            assert mask.any().item() == (
                normalized_label(record) == "tampered"
            )
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": normalized_label(record),
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            positive_pixels += mask.sum().item()
            total_pixels += mask.numel()
        return digest.hexdigest(), 100 * positive_pixels / total_pixels
    train_hash, train_fg = fingerprint(train_subset, train_records)
    val_hash, val_fg = fingerprint(val_subset, val_records)
    print(f"Train: 256 images | GT foreground: {train_fg:.4f}%")
    print(f"Val:   128 images | GT foreground: {val_fg:.4f}%")
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Balanced train sampling: all 256 images once per epoch.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    class BalancedBatchSampler(Sampler):
        def __init__(self):
            self.epoch = 1
        def __len__(self):
            return 32
        def __iter__(self):
            rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + self.epoch)
            auth = rng.permutation(train_auth)
            tamp = rng.permutation(train_tamp)
            batches = []
            for start in range(0, 128, 4):
                batch = np.concatenate([
                    auth[start:start + 4],
                    tamp[start:start + 4],
                ])
                rng.shuffle(batch)
                batches.append(batch.tolist())
            rng.shuffle(batches)
            yield from batches

=== PAGE 146 ===
    sampler = BalancedBatchSampler()
    training_loader = DataLoader(
        train_subset,
        batch_sampler=sampler,
        num_workers=0,
        pin_memory=device.type == "cuda",
    )
    train_eval_loader = DataLoader(

    validation_loader = DataLoader(

    config = {

    random.seed(SEED)
    np.random.seed(SEED)
    torch.manual_seed(SEED)
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
    model = UNet(
        in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
    ).to(device)
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4
    )
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_fn(logits, targets):

=== PAGE 147 ===
        logits, targets = logits.float(), targets.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
        )
        return bce + dice_loss
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(loader, records, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        weighted_loss = samples = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_fp = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        offset = 0
        for images, masks in loader:
            images = images.to(device)
            masks = masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images)
            loss = loss_fn(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation loss.")
            weighted_loss += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
            samples += images.shape[0]
            pred = logits.sigmoid() > THRESHOLD
            truth = masks.bool()
            for i in range(images.shape[0]):
                record = records[offset + i]
                p, gt = pred[i], truth[i]
                itp = (p & gt).sum().item()
                ifp = (p & ~gt).sum().item()
                ifn = (~p & gt).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                tampered = normalized_label(record) == "tampered"
                if tampered:
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += p.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += p.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": normalized_label(record),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * p.float().mean().item(),
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            offset += images.shape[0]
        tampered_dice = [
            r["dice_05"] for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"
        ]
        metrics = {
            # Loss is image-weighted mean of batch objectives.
            "loss": weighted_loss / samples,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": float(np.mean(tampered_dice)),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(d < .1 for d in tampered_dice),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),

=== PAGE 148 ===
        }
        return metrics, details
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_atomic(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    history, evaluations, per_image = [], [], []
    best_score, best_epoch, best_state = -1.0, None, None
    start_epoch = 1
    if saved is not None:
        model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
        optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
        history = saved["history"]
        evaluations = saved["evaluations"]
        per_image = saved["per_image"]
        best_score = saved["best_score"]
        best_epoch = saved["best_epoch"]
        best_state = saved["best_model_state"]
        start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
        torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
        if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
            torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
        del saved
        print("Resume from epoch:", start_epoch - 1)
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        for epoch in range(start_epoch, TOTAL_EPOCHS + 1):
            sampler.epoch = epoch
            model.train()
            total_loss = total_images = 0
            for images, masks in training_loader:
                images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                logits = model(images)
                loss = loss_fn(logits, masks)
                if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite loss at epoch {epoch}")
                loss.backward()
                for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                    if not parameter.requires_grad:
                        continue
                    if parameter.grad is None:
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                    if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                optimizer.step()
                total_loss += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
                total_images += images.shape[0]
            history.append({

=== PAGE 149 ===
                "epoch": epoch,
                "optimizer_steps": epoch * 32,
                "training_loss_online": total_loss / total_images,
            })
            message = (
                f"Epoch {epoch:02d}/{TOTAL_EPOCHS} | "
                f"Steps {epoch * 32:04d} | "
                f"Online loss {total_loss / total_images:.4f}"
            )
            if epoch == 1 or epoch % EVAL_EVERY == 0 or epoch == TOTAL_EPOCHS:
                train_metrics, train_details = evaluate(
                    train_eval_loader, train_records, epoch, "train"
                )
                val_metrics, val_details = evaluate(
                    validation_loader, val_records, epoch, "validation"
                )
                evaluations.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    **{f"train_{k}": v for k, v in train_metrics.items()},
                    **{f"val_{k}": v for k, v in val_metrics.items()},
                })
                per_image.extend(train_details + val_details)
                score = val_metrics["mean_tampered_dice"]
                if score > best_score:
                    best_score, best_epoch = score, epoch
                    best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                message += (
                    f" | Train Micro {train_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f}"
                    f" | Val Micro {val_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f}"
                    f" | Val Mean Tp {score:.4f}"
                    f" | Val Dice<0.1 "
                    f"{val_metrics['tampered_dice_below_01']}/64"
                )
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            # Save every epoch; last.pt contains enough state to resume.
            save_atomic({
                "kind": "train256_val128_v1",
                "epoch": epoch,
                "config": config,
                "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                "best_model_state": best_state,
                "best_score": best_score,
                "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                "history": history,
                "evaluations": evaluations,
                "per_image": per_image,
                "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                "cuda_rng": (
                    torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                    if device.type == "cuda" else None
                ),
            }, run_dir / "last.pt")
            save_atomic({
                "kind": "train256_val128_best_weights",
                "epoch": best_epoch,
                "config": config,
                "validation_mean_tampered_dice": best_score,
                "model_state": best_state,
            }, run_dir / "best_val_macro.pt")
            pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                run_dir / "history.csv", index=False
            )
            pd.DataFrame(evaluations).to_csv(
                run_dir / "evaluations.csv", index=False
            )
            pd.DataFrame(per_image).to_csv(
                run_dir / "per_image.csv", index=False

=== PAGE 150 ===
            )
            print(message + " | SAVED", flush=True)
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_deterministic
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    model.eval()
    print("\nBest validation mean tampered Dice:", best_score)
    print("Selected epoch:", best_epoch)
    print("Resume:", run_dir / "last.pt")
    print("Best weights:", run_dir / "best_val_macro.pt")
    return model, pd.DataFrame(evaluations), run_dir

def diagnose_epoch50():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Chạy startup trước để tạo train_dataset và val_dataset."
        )
    checkpoint_path = RUN_DIR / "last.pt"
    saved = torch.load(
        checkpoint_path,

        raise RuntimeError("Checkpoint không thuộc thí nghiệm TRAIN256/VAL128.")

            f"Checkpoint đang ở epoch {saved.get('epoch')}, không phải 50."
        )

        "base_channels": 16,

        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_batch_global_dice_smooth1",
    }

            raise RuntimeError(f"Cấu hình checkpoint không khớp: {key}")
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
