    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(model, loader, records, include_dice, branch, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        # Preallocate CPU arrays. Raw logits preserve ranking without
        # sigmoid saturation; AP is invariant under strictly monotone maps.

=== PAGE 208 ===
        n_pixels = len(records) * 256 * 256
        scores = np.empty(n_pixels, dtype=np.float32)
        targets_cpu = np.empty(n_pixels, dtype=np.bool_)
        pixel_offset = image_offset = 0
        bce_sum = foreground_bce_sum = background_bce_sum = 0.0
        batch_dice_sum = 0.0
        foreground_probability = background_probability = 0.0
        fg_pixels = bg_pixels = sample_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = auth_fp = auth_pixels = pred_pixels = 0
        max_probability = 0.0
        details = []
        for images, masks in loader:
            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images).float()
            if not torch.isfinite(logits).all().item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite logits.")
            truth = masks.bool()
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            pred = probs > .5
            pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
            bce_map = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits, masks, pos_weight=pos_weight, reduction="none"
            )
            bce_sum += bce_map.double().sum().item()
            foreground_bce_sum += bce_map[truth].double().sum().item()
            background_bce_sum += bce_map[~truth].double().sum().item()
            intersection = (probs * masks).sum().item()
            denominator = probs.sum().item() + masks.sum().item()
            batch_dice_loss = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
            )
            batch_dice_sum += batch_dice_loss * images.shape[0]
            sample_count += images.shape[0]
            fg_pixels += truth.sum().item()
            bg_pixels += (~truth).sum().item()
            foreground_probability += probs[truth].double().sum().item()
            background_probability += probs[~truth].double().sum().item()
            max_probability = max(max_probability, probs.max().item())
            pred_pixels += pred.sum().item()
            length = logits.numel()
            scores[pixel_offset:pixel_offset + length] = (
                logits.cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)
            )
            targets_cpu[pixel_offset:pixel_offset + length] = (
                truth.cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)
            )
            pixel_offset += length
            for j in range(images.shape[0]):
                record = records[image_offset + j]
                p, gt = pred[j], truth[j]
                itp = (p & gt).sum().item()
                ifp = (p & ~gt).sum().item()
                ifn = (~p & gt).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                if label(record) == "tampered":
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += p.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += p.numel()
                details.append({
                    "branch": branch, "epoch": epoch, "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": label(record),
                    "dice_05": dice,

=== PAGE 209 ===
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * p.float().mean().item(),
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            image_offset += images.shape[0]
        assert pixel_offset == n_pixels
        print(f"  {branch} epoch {epoch}: calculating {split} pixel AP...",
              flush=True)
        ap = float(average_precision_score(targets_cpu, scores))
        del scores, targets_cpu
        gc.collect()
        dices = [r["dice_05"] for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        bce = bce_sum / n_pixels
        dice_loss = batch_dice_sum / sample_count
        metrics = {
            "objective_loss": bce + (dice_loss if include_dice else 0),
            "weighted_bce": bce,
            # These two are conditional means, not additive components.
            "foreground_bce_mean": foreground_bce_sum / fg_pixels,
            "background_bce_mean": background_bce_sum / bg_pixels,
            "dice_loss_reference": dice_loss,
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice_05": float(np.mean(dices)),
            "precision_05": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall_05": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(d < .1 for d in dices),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * pred_pixels / n_pixels,
            "prob_in_mask": foreground_probability / fg_pixels,
            "prob_background": background_probability / bg_pixels,
            "max_probability": max_probability,
            "pixel_AP": ap,
            "foreground_prevalence_AP_baseline": fg_pixels / n_pixels,
        }
        return metrics, details
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    combined = []
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        for branch, include_dice in BRANCHES.items():
            branch_dir = output / branch
            branch_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            last_path = branch_dir / "last.pt"
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):

                    in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16

                    model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4

                pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
                history, per_image, online_history = [], [], []
                best_score, best_epoch, best_state = -1.0, None, None
                start_epoch = 0
                if last_path.exists():

                    saved = torch.load(
                        last_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True

def run_seed_study():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy startup trước.")
    if not Path("/content/drive/MyDrive").is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError("Google Drive chưa được mount.")
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_atomic(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    if RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR is None:
        output = OUTPUT_ROOT / (
            "seed_study_" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        for filename in ("train256.csv", "validation128.csv"):
            (output / filename).write_bytes(
                (SOURCE_DIR / filename).read_bytes()
            )
    else:
        output = Path(RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR)
    def label(record):
        value = str(record["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    def restore(dataset, filename, count):
        table = pd.read_csv(output / filename, keep_default_na=False)
        indices = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == count
        records = []
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(f"Selection mismatch: {filename}")

=== PAGE 215 ===
            records.append(record)
        return Subset(dataset, indices), records
    train_subset, train_records = restore(
        train_dataset, "train256.csv", 256
    )
    val_subset, val_records = restore(
        val_dataset, "validation128.csv", 128
    )
    for field in ("image_path", "mask_path", "source_group", "image_hash"):
        def values(records):
            result = set()
            for record in records:
                if field not in record:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing field: {field}")
                value = record[field]
                if value is None or str(value) == "":
                    continue
                result.add(
                    str(Path(value).resolve())
                    if field.endswith("_path") else str(value)
                )
            return result
        if values(train_records) & values(val_records):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Cross-split overlap: {field}")
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            assert mask.any().item() == (label(record) == "tampered")
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": label(record),
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
        return digest.hexdigest()
    train_hash = fingerprint(train_subset, train_records)
    val_hash = fingerprint(val_subset, val_records)

    ]

    schedules = {}
    for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):

    # Hash the schedule rule, allowing an interrupted run to resume.
    config = {

        "weight_decay": 1e-4,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,

        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_batch_global_dice_smooth1",

        "val": DataLoader(
            val_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
        ),
    }
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
        return bce + dice_loss, bce, dice_loss
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(model, loader, records, captured, seed, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        n_pixels = len(records) * 256 * 256
        scores = np.empty(n_pixels, np.float32)
        truth_cpu = np.empty(n_pixels, np.bool_)
        pixel_offset = image_offset = 0

        samples = fg_pixels = bg_pixels = 0
        tp = fp = fn = pred_pixels = auth_fp = auth_pixels = 0
        prob_fg = prob_bg = 0.0
        max_prob = 0.0
        channel_active_pixels = np.zeros(16, np.int64)
        channel_active_images = np.zeros(16, np.int64)

        activation_max = 0.0
        positive_fg = positive_bg = negative_fg = negative_bg = 0.0
        details = []
        head_weights = model.head.weight.detach()[0, :, 0, 0]
        for images, masks in loader:
            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images).float()
            activation = captured.pop("activation")
            loss, bce, dice_loss = loss_parts(logits, masks)
            if not (
                torch.isfinite(loss).item()
                and torch.isfinite(activation).all().item()
            ):
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation values.")
            n = images.shape[0]
            samples += n
            loss_sum += loss.item() * n
            bce_sum += bce.item() * n
            dice_sum += dice_loss.item() * n
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            gt = masks.bool()
            pred = probs > .5
            fg_pixels += gt.sum().item()
            bg_pixels += (~gt).sum().item()
            pred_pixels += pred.sum().item()
            prob_fg += probs[gt].double().sum().item()
            prob_bg += probs[~gt].double().sum().item()
            max_prob = max(max_prob, probs.max().item())
            channel_active_pixels += (
                (activation != 0).sum((0, 2, 3)).cpu().numpy()
            )
            channel_active_images += (
                (activation != 0).flatten(2).any(2).sum(0).cpu().numpy()
            )
            activation_max = max(activation_max, activation.max().item())
            contribution = activation * head_weights[None, :, None, None]
            positive_map = contribution.clamp_min(0).sum(1)
            negative_map = contribution.clamp_max(0).sum(1)
            foreground = gt[:, 0]
            positive_fg += positive_map[foreground].double().sum().item()
            positive_bg += positive_map[~foreground].double().sum().item()
            negative_fg += negative_map[foreground].double().sum().item()
            negative_bg += negative_map[~foreground].double().sum().item()
            length = logits.numel()
            scores[pixel_offset:pixel_offset + length] = (
                logits.cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)
            )
            truth_cpu[pixel_offset:pixel_offset + length] = (
                gt.cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)
            )
            pixel_offset += length
            for j in range(n):
                record = records[image_offset + j]
                p, truth = pred[j], gt[j]
                itp = (p & truth).sum().item()
                ifp = (p & ~truth).sum().item()
                ifn = (~p & truth).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                if label(record) == "tampered":
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += p.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += p.numel()

=== PAGE 218 ===
                details.append({
                    "seed": seed, "epoch": epoch, "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": label(record),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * p.float().mean().item(),
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            image_offset += n
        assert pixel_offset == n_pixels
        print(f"  Seed {seed}, epoch {epoch}: computing {split} AP...",
              flush=True)
        ap = float(average_precision_score(truth_cpu, scores))

            "precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),

            "pixel_AP": ap,

            "prob_in_mask": prob_fg / fg_pixels,
            "prob_background": prob_bg / bg_pixels,
            "max_probability": max_prob,
            "activation_zero_pct": (
                100 * (1 - channel_active_pixels.sum() / (16 * n_pixels))

            "channels_zero_all_images": int((channel_active_images == 0).sum()),
            "activation_max": activation_max,

            "negative_contribution_mask": negative_fg / fg_pixels,
            "positive_contribution_background": positive_bg / bg_pixels,

            "images_active": int(channel_active_images[channel]),

# SYNTHETIC SEGMENTATION SANITY CHECK
# TRAIN: 256 images (128 empty + 128 rectangle)

OUTPUT_ROOT = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics"
)
EPOCHS = 20
# First run: None.

RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR = None
class SyntheticRectangles(Dataset):
    """Clearly labeled synthetic fixtures; no real images or annotations."""
    def __init__(self, count, seed):
        assert count % 2 == 0
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.images = np.empty((count, 256, 256, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        self.masks = np.zeros((count, 256, 256), dtype=np.uint8)
        for i in range(count):
            # Dark background, varying by image and RGB channel.
            base = rng.integers(20, 85, size=(1, 1, 3))
            noise = rng.normal(0, 5, size=(256, 256, 3))
            image = np.clip(base + noise, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
            if i >= count // 2:
                # About 2–16% foreground on positive images.
                height = int(rng.integers(36, 103))
                width = int(rng.integers(36, 103))
                top = int(rng.integers(0, 257 - height))
                left = int(rng.integers(0, 257 - width))
                color = rng.integers(175, 246, size=(1, 1, 3))
                patch_noise = rng.normal(0, 4, size=(height, width, 3))
                image[top:top + height, left:left + width] = np.clip(
                    color + patch_noise, 0, 255
                ).astype(np.uint8)
                self.masks[i, top:top + height, left:left + width] = 1
            self.images[i] = image
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.images)
    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image = torch.from_numpy(
            self.images[index].transpose(2, 0, 1).copy()
        ).float() / 255.0
        mask = torch.from_numpy(
            self.masks[index][None].copy()
        ).float()
        return image, mask

    def balanced_batches(epoch):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(config["batch_schedule_seed"] + epoch)
        empty = rng.permutation(np.arange(128))
        positive = rng.permutation(np.arange(128, 256))
        batches = []
        for start in range(0, 128, 4):
            batch = np.concatenate([
                empty[start:start + 4], positive[start:start + 4]
            ])
            rng.shuffle(batch)
            batches.append(batch.tolist())
        rng.shuffle(batches)
        assert sorted(i for batch in batches for i in batch) == list(range(256))
        return batches
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def atomic_save(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probabilities * targets).sum()
        denominator = probabilities.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
        return bce + dice_loss, bce, dice_loss

=== PAGE 225 ===
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(model, loader):
        model.eval()
        loss_sum = bce_sum = dice_sum = 0.0
        samples = tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_fp = auth_pixels = fg_pixels = bg_pixels = 0
        probability_fg = probability_bg = 0.0
        max_probability = 0.0
        per_positive_dice = []
        for images, masks in loader:
            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images)
            loss, bce, dice_loss = loss_parts(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation loss.")
            n = images.shape[0]
            samples += n
            loss_sum += loss.item() * n
            bce_sum += bce.item() * n
            dice_sum += dice_loss.item() * n
            probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
            pred = probabilities > .5
            gt = masks.bool()
            tp += (pred & gt).sum().item()
            fp += (pred & ~gt).sum().item()
            fn += (~pred & gt).sum().item()
            fg_pixels += gt.sum().item()
            bg_pixels += (~gt).sum().item()
            probability_fg += probabilities[gt].double().sum().item()
            probability_bg += probabilities[~gt].double().sum().item()
            max_probability = max(max_probability, probabilities.max().item())
            for j in range(n):
                if gt[j].any().item():
                    numerator = 2 * (pred[j] & gt[j]).sum().item()
                    denominator = pred[j].sum().item() + gt[j].sum().item()
                    per_positive_dice.append(numerator / denominator)
                else:
                    auth_fp += pred[j].sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += pred[j].numel()
        return {
            "loss": loss_sum / samples,
            "bce": bce_sum / samples,
            "dice_loss": dice_sum / samples,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "mean_positive_image_dice": float(np.mean(per_positive_dice)),
            "precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "positive_images_dice_below_01": sum(
                d < .1 for d in per_positive_dice
            ),
            "empty_image_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "prob_in_mask": probability_fg / fg_pixels,
            "prob_background": probability_bg / bg_pixels,
            "max_probability": max_probability,
        }
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    history = []
    best_score, best_epoch, best_state = -1., None, None
    start_epoch = 0
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True

            for epoch in range(start_epoch, EPOCHS + 1):
                online_loss = np.nan
                if epoch > 0:
                    model.train()
                    loader = DataLoader(
                        train_ds,
                        batch_sampler=balanced_batches(epoch),
                        num_workers=0,
                        pin_memory=device.type == "cuda",
                    )
                    loss_sum = count = 0
                    for images, masks in loader:
                        images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                        logits = model(images)
                        loss, _, _ = loss_parts(logits, masks)
                        if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss.")
                        loss.backward()
                        for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                            if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                continue
                            if parameter.grad is None:
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                            if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                        optimizer.step()
                        loss_sum += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
                        count += images.shape[0]
                    online_loss = loss_sum / count
                tm = evaluate(model, train_eval_loader)
                vm = evaluate(model, val_loader_synthetic)
                history.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,

=== PAGE 227 ===
                    "optimizer_steps": epoch * 32,
                    "online_loss": online_loss,
                    **{f"train_{k}": v for k, v in tm.items()},
                    **{f"val_{k}": v for k, v in vm.items()},
                })
                score = vm["mean_positive_image_dice"]
                if score > best_score:
                    best_score, best_epoch = score, epoch
                    best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                atomic_save({
                    "kind": "synthetic_rectangles_v1",
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "config": config,
                    "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                    "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                    "history": history,
                    "best_score": best_score,
                    "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                    "best_model_state": best_state,
                    "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                    "cuda_rng": (
                        torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                        if device.type == "cuda" else None
                    ),
                }, output / "last.pt")
                atomic_save({
                    "kind": "synthetic_rectangles_best_weights",
                    "epoch": best_epoch,
                    "config": config,
                    "synthetic_validation_mean_dice": best_score,
                    "model_state": best_state,
                }, output / "best_synthetic.pt")
                pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                    output / "history.csv", index=False
                )
                print(
                    f"SYNTHETIC | Epoch {epoch:02d}/{EPOCHS} | "
                    f"Train Dice {tm['micro_dice']:.4f} | "
                    f"Val Dice {vm['micro_dice']:.4f} | "
                    f"Val Mean Dice {score:.4f} | "
                    f"Val empty FP {vm['empty_image_fp_pct']:.3f}% | SAVED",
                    flush=True,
                )
            model.eval()
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_det
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    # Display unseen synthetic validation examples.
    chosen = [0, 1, 64, 65, 66, 67]
    example_images = torch.stack([val_ds[i][0] for i in chosen])
    example_masks = torch.stack([val_ds[i][1] for i in chosen])
    with torch.inference_mode():
        probabilities = model(example_images.to(device)).sigmoid().cpu()
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(len(chosen), 4, figsize=(12, 16))
    for row, index in enumerate(chosen):
        panels = [
            example_images[row].permute(1, 2, 0),
            example_masks[row, 0],
            probabilities[row, 0],
            probabilities[row, 0] > .5,
        ]
        titles = [
            f"SYNTHETIC VAL {index}",

=== PAGE 228 ===
            "Ground truth",
            "Probability 0–1",
            "Prediction > 0.5",
        ]
        for col, (panel, title) in enumerate(zip(panels, titles)):
            if col == 0:
                axes[row, col].imshow(panel)
            else:
                axes[row, col].imshow(
                    panel,
                    cmap="viridis" if col == 2 else "gray",
                    vmin=0, vmax=1,
                )
            axes[row, col].set_title(title)
            axes[row, col].axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(output / "synthetic_validation_examples.png", dpi=150)
    plt.show()
    return model, pd.DataFrame(history), output
# Free GPU memory of earlier diagnostic models.
for name in ("model256", "diag32_model", "evaluation_model", "synthetic_model"):
    if name in globals():
        globals()[name].cpu()
gc.collect()
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
synthetic_model, synthetic_history, synthetic_dir = run_synthetic_check()

"""Lightweight U-Net for pixel-level image forgery localization."""
import torch
from torch import Tensor, nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
class DoubleConv(nn.Module):
    """Two padded convolutions learn features without changing image size."""
    def __init__(self, in_channels: int, out_channels: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.layers = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=3, padding=1),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
            nn.Conv2d(out_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=3, padding=1),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
        )
    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        return self.layers(x)
class UpBlock(nn.Module):
    """Upsample coarse features and combine them with an encoder skip."""
    def __init__(self, in_channels: int, skip_channels: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        # Reduce channels before combining with the high-resolution skip.
        self.reduce = nn.Conv2d(in_channels, skip_channels, kernel_size=1)
        self.conv = DoubleConv(2 * skip_channels, skip_channels)
    def forward(self, x: Tensor, skip: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        x = self.reduce(x)
        # Match the skip exactly, including for odd input heights/widths.
        x = F.interpolate(x, size=skip.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        x = torch.cat((skip, x), dim=1)  # Concatenate along the channel axis.
        return self.conv(x)
class UNet(nn.Module):
    """Four-level U-Net returning raw, full-resolution segmentation logits.
    Defaults map (B, 3, H, W) RGB images to (B, 1, H, W) binary logits.
    Height and width must each be at least 16 for four pooling operations.
    A base width of 16 keeps the model small; channels grow to 256 at the
    bottleneck. No pretrained weights or device selection are implicit.
    """

=== PAGE 234 ===
    def __init__(
        self, in_channels: int = 3, out_channels: int = 1, base_channels: int = 16
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        for name, value in (("in channels", in channels), ("out channels", out channels),

        prediction = probabilities >= 0.5
        target = mask[0] >= 0.5
        label = val_dataset.records[index]["label"]
        tp = (prediction & target).sum().item()
        fp = (prediction & ~target).sum().item()
        fn = (~prediction & target).sum().item()
        pred_area = 100 * prediction.float().mean().item()
        if label == "tampered":
            dice = 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1)
            metric_text = f"Dice={dice:.4f}"
        else:
            metric_text = f"FP pixels={pred_area:.2f}%"
        print(
            f"Index {index} | {label} | "
            f"Prob min/max: "
            f"{probabilities.min().item():.4f}/"
            f"{probabilities.max().item():.4f} | "
            f"Pred area: {pred_area:.2f}% | "
            f"{metric_text}"
        )

    val_dataset,

    state = torch.load(
        path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
    )
    eval_model.load_state_dict(state["model"], strict=True)
    eval_model.eval()
    stats = {
        t: {
            "tp": 0, "fp": 0, "fn": 0,
            "tampered_dice": [],
            "auth_fp": 0, "auth_pixels": 0,
        }
        for t in thresholds
    }
    offset = 0
    max_probability = 0.0
    with torch.inference_mode():
        for images, masks in tqdm(audit_loader, desc=filename):
            probs = eval_model(
                images.to(eval_device)
            ).sigmoid().cpu()[:, 0]
            assert torch.isfinite(probs).all()
            targets = masks[:, 0] >= 0.5
            max_probability = max(
                max_probability, probs.max().item()
            )
            records = val_dataset.records[
                offset:offset + len(images)
            ]
            is_tampered = torch.tensor([
                r["label"] == "tampered" for r in records
            ])
            offset += len(images)
            for threshold in thresholds:
                pred = probs >= threshold
                # TP/FP/FN riêng cho từng ảnh.
                tp = (pred & targets).sum(dim=(1, 2))
                fp = (pred & ~targets).sum(dim=(1, 2))
                fn = (~pred & targets).sum(dim=(1, 2))
                s = stats[threshold]
                s["tp"] += tp.sum().item()
                s["fp"] += fp.sum().item()
                s["fn"] += fn.sum().item()
                dice = (
                    2 * tp.float()
                    / (2 * tp + fp + fn).clamp_min(1)
                )
                s["tampered_dice"].extend(
                    dice[is_tampered].tolist()
                )
                authentic_pred = pred[~is_tampered]
                s["auth_fp"] += authentic_pred.sum().item()
                s["auth_pixels"] += authentic_pred.numel()
    assert offset == len(val_dataset)
    for threshold, s in stats.items():
        tp, fp, fn = s["tp"], s["fp"], s["fn"]
        results.append({
            "checkpoint": filename,
            "epoch": state.get("epoch"),
            "threshold": threshold,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "micro_iou": tp / max(tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "pixel_precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),

=== PAGE 241 ===
            "pixel_recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": (
                np.mean(s["tampered_dice"])
                if s["tampered_dice"] else float("nan")
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pixel_pct": (
                100 * s["auth_fp"] / max(s["auth_pixels"], 1)
            ),
            "max_probability": max_probability,
        })
    del state

def split_signature(dataset):
    fields = [
        "image_hash", "label", "source_group",
        "mask_verified", "mask_source",
        "mask_threshold", "threshold_approved",
    ]
    records = [
        {
            **{key: str(row.get(key, "")) for key in fields},
            "mask_name": (
                Path(row["mask_path"]).name
                if row["mask_path"] is not None else ""
            ),
        }
        for row in dataset.records
    ]
    text = json.dumps(records, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
class R34Dataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, base, augment=False):
        self.base = base
        self.augment = augment
        self.mean = torch.tensor(

=== PAGE 243 ===
            [0.485, 0.456, 0.406]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)
        self.std = torch.tensor(
            [0.229, 0.224, 0.225]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.base)
    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image, mask = self.base[index]
        if self.augment:
            if torch.rand(()).item() < 0.5:
                image = image.flip(-1)
                mask = mask.flip(-1)
            if torch.rand(()).item() < 0.5:
                image = image.flip(-2)
                mask = mask.flip(-2)
        image = (image - self.mean) / self.std
        return (
            image.contiguous(),
            mask.float().contiguous(),
        )

import torch.nn.functional as F
r34_last_path = r34_dir / "last.pt"
if (
    not r34_last_path.exists()
    and list(r34_dir.glob("*.pt"))

    raise RuntimeError(
        "Thư mục có checkpoint khác nhưng thiếu last.pt. "

=== PAGE 244 ===
        "Kiểm tra trước khi bắt đầu."

r34_model = smp.Unet(
    encoder_name="resnet34",
    encoder_weights=(
        None if r34_last_path.exists() else "imagenet"
    ),
    in_channels=3,
    classes=1,
    activation=None,
).to(r34_device)
r34_optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
    r34_model.parameters(),
    lr=r34_config["learning_rate"],
    weight_decay=r34_config["weight_decay"],
)
r34_scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda")
r34_pos_weight = torch.tensor(
    [r34_config["pos_weight"]],
    device=r34_device,
)
def r34_loss_fn(logits, masks):
    # Loss được tính bằng float32 dù forward dùng mixed precision.
    logits = logits.float()
    masks = masks.float()
    bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        logits,
        masks,
        pos_weight=r34_pos_weight,
    )
    probs = logits.sigmoid()
    intersection = (probs * masks).sum()
    denominator = probs.sum() + masks.sum()
    dice_loss = 1 - (
        (2 * intersection + 1.0)
        / (denominator + 1.0)
    )
    return bce + dice_loss
r34_model.eval()
with torch.inference_mode():
    check_images, check_masks = next(iter(r34_val_loader))
    check_images = check_images.to(r34_device)
    check_masks = check_masks.to(r34_device)
    assert torch.isfinite(check_images).all()
    assert ((check_masks == 0) | (check_masks == 1)).all()
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
        check_logits = r34_model(check_images)
        check_loss = r34_loss_fn(check_logits, check_masks)
    assert check_logits.shape == check_masks.shape
    assert torch.isfinite(check_loss)
print("Output shape:", tuple(check_logits.shape))
print("Loss kiểm tra:", round(check_loss.item(), 4))
print(
    "Số tham số:",
    f"{sum(p.numel() for p in r34_model.parameters()):,}",
)
print("PASS — chưa cập nhật trọng số.")

import math
from tqdm.auto import tqdm
def r34_save(state, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    torch.save(state, temporary)
    temporary.replace(path)
def r34_save_csv(rows, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(temporary, index=False)
    temporary.replace(path)
def r34_train_epoch():
    r34_model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    total_samples = 0
    tp = fp = fn = 0
    for images, masks in tqdm(
        r34_train_loader, desc="Train", leave=False
    ):
        images = images.to(r34_device, non_blocking=True)
        masks = masks.to(r34_device, non_blocking=True)
        r34_optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
            logits = r34_model(images)
            loss = r34_loss_fn(logits, masks)
        if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
            raise RuntimeError("Train loss NaN/Inf; dừng epoch.")
        r34_scaler.scale(loss).backward()
        r34_scaler.step(r34_optimizer)
        r34_scaler.update()
        total_loss += loss.item() * len(images)
        total_samples += len(images)
        with torch.no_grad():
            pred = logits.float().sigmoid() >= 0.5
            target = masks >= 0.5
            tp += (pred & target).sum().item()
            fp += (pred & ~target).sum().item()
            fn += (~pred & target).sum().item()
    return {
        "train_loss": total_loss / total_samples,
        "train_online_dice_05": (
            2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1)
        ),
    }
def r34_validate():
    r34_model.eval()
    stats = {
        t: {
            "tp": 0, "fp": 0, "fn": 0,
            "dice_sum": 0.0, "tampered_count": 0,

=== PAGE 246 ===
            "auth_fp": 0, "auth_pixels": 0,
        }
        for t in r34_config["thresholds"]
    }
    total_loss = 0.0
    total_samples = 0
    max_probability = 0.0
    with torch.inference_mode():
        for images, masks in tqdm(
            r34_val_loader, desc="Validation", leave=False
        ):
            images = images.to(r34_device, non_blocking=True)
            masks = masks.to(r34_device, non_blocking=True)
            with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                logits = r34_model(images)
                loss = r34_loss_fn(logits, masks)
            probs = logits.float().sigmoid()
            if (
                not torch.isfinite(loss).item()
                or not torch.isfinite(probs).all().item()
            ):
                raise RuntimeError("Validation chứa NaN/Inf.")
            target = masks >= 0.5
            records = val_dataset.records[
                total_samples:total_samples + len(images)
            ]
            is_tampered = torch.tensor(
                [r["label"] == "tampered" for r in records],
                dtype=torch.bool,
                device=r34_device,
            )
            # Kiểm tra nhãn và mask của toàn validation.
            has_foreground = target.flatten(1).any(dim=1)
            assert torch.equal(has_foreground, is_tampered)
            total_loss += loss.item() * len(images)
            total_samples += len(images)
            max_probability = max(
                max_probability, probs.max().item()
            )
            for threshold, s in stats.items():
                pred = probs >= threshold
                dims = (1, 2, 3)
                tp = (pred & target).sum(dim=dims)
                fp = (pred & ~target).sum(dim=dims)
                fn = (~pred & target).sum(dim=dims)
                s["tp"] += tp.sum().item()
                s["fp"] += fp.sum().item()
                s["fn"] += fn.sum().item()
                dice = (
                    2 * tp.float()
                    / (2 * tp + fp + fn).clamp_min(1)
                )
                s["dice_sum"] += dice[is_tampered].sum().item()
                s["tampered_count"] += is_tampered.sum().item()
                authentic_pred = pred[~is_tampered]
                s["auth_fp"] += authentic_pred.sum().item()
                s["auth_pixels"] += authentic_pred.numel()
    assert total_samples == len(val_dataset)
    rows = []
    for threshold, s in stats.items():

=== PAGE 247 ===
        tp, fp, fn = s["tp"], s["fp"], s["fn"]
        rows.append({
            "threshold": threshold,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "micro_iou": tp / max(tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "pixel_precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "pixel_recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": (
                s["dice_sum"] / max(s["tampered_count"], 1)
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pixel_pct": (
                100 * s["auth_fp"] / max(s["auth_pixels"], 1)
            ),
            "max_probability": max_probability,
        })
    return total_loss / total_samples, rows
# Khôi phục cả model, optimizer, scaler và RNG ở mỗi lần chạy cell.
r34_history = []
r34_threshold_history = []
r34_best_dice = -1.0
r34_best_dice_loss = math.inf
r34_best_loss = math.inf
r34_start_epoch = 1
if r34_last_path.exists():
    state = torch.load(
        r34_last_path,
        map_location="cpu",
        weights_only=True,
    )
    if state["config"] != r34_config:
        raise RuntimeError("Checkpoint không khớp cấu hình.")
    r34_model.load_state_dict(state["model"])
    r34_optimizer.load_state_dict(state["optimizer"])
    r34_scaler.load_state_dict(state["scaler"])

    r34_threshold_history = state["threshold_history"]
    r34_best_dice = state["best_dice"]
    r34_best_dice_loss = state["best_dice_loss"]

    torch.set_rng_state(state["torch_rng"])
    torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all(state["cuda_rng"])
    r34_train_generator.set_state(state["train_loader_rng"])
    r34_val_generator.set_state(state["val_loader_rng"])
    del state
    print("Resume từ epoch:", r34_start_epoch)
else:
    print("Bắt đầu thí nghiệm mới từ epoch 1.")
for epoch in range(r34_start_epoch, r34_config["epochs"] + 1):
    train_metrics = r34_train_epoch()
    val_loss, threshold_rows = r34_validate()
    val05 = next(
        row for row in threshold_rows
        if row["threshold"] == 0.5
    )
    score = val05["micro_dice"]
    improved_dice = (
        score > r34_best_dice
        or (
            score == r34_best_dice
            and val_loss < r34_best_dice_loss
        )
    )
    improved_loss = val_loss < r34_best_loss

=== PAGE 248 ===
    if improved_dice:
        r34_best_dice = score
        r34_best_dice_loss = val_loss
    if improved_loss:
        r34_best_loss = val_loss
    r34_history.append({
        "epoch": epoch,
        **train_metrics,
        "val_loss": val_loss,
        **{
            f"val_{key}": value
            for key, value in val05.items()
            if key != "threshold"
        },
    })
    r34_threshold_history.extend(
        {"epoch": epoch, **row}
        for row in threshold_rows
    )
    state = {
        "epoch": epoch,
        "config": r34_config,
        "model": r34_model.state_dict(),
        "optimizer": r34_optimizer.state_dict(),
        "scaler": r34_scaler.state_dict(),
        "best_dice": r34_best_dice,
        "best_dice_loss": r34_best_dice_loss,
        "best_loss": r34_best_loss,
        "history": r34_history,
        "threshold_history": r34_threshold_history,
        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
        "cuda_rng": torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all(),
        "train_loader_rng": r34_train_generator.get_state(),
        "val_loader_rng": r34_val_generator.get_state(),
    }
    # last.pt là nguồn trạng thái để tiếp tục chạy.
    r34_save(state, r34_last_path)
    if improved_dice:
        r34_save(state, r34_dir / "best_dice.pt")
    if improved_loss:
        r34_save(state, r34_dir / "best_loss.pt")
    r34_save_csv(r34_history, r34_dir / "history.csv")
    r34_save_csv(
        r34_threshold_history,
        r34_dir / "validation_thresholds.csv",
    )

        f"Epoch {epoch:02d}/{r34_config['epochs']} | "
        f"Train loss {train_metrics['train_loss']:.4f} | "
        f"Val loss {val_loss:.4f} | "

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sample_rows = []

    _, mask = val_dataset[index]
    record = val_dataset.records[index]

                        actual_area - metadata["gt_area_pct"]

# Cùng cách tính signature với baseline RGB.
def noise_split_signature(dataset):
    fields = [
        "image_hash", "label", "source_group",
        "mask_verified", "mask_source",
        "mask_threshold", "threshold_approved",
    ]
    records = [
        {
            **{k: str(row.get(k, "")) for k in fields},

=== PAGE 260 ===
            "mask_name": (
                Path(row["mask_path"]).name
                if row["mask_path"] is not None else ""
            ),
        }
        for row in dataset.records
    ]
    text = json.dumps(records, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
assert noise_split_signature(train_dataset) == baseline_config["train_signature"]
assert noise_split_signature(val_dataset) == baseline_config["val_signature"]

assert str(smp.__version__) == baseline_config["smp_version"]

import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
class RGBNoiseDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, base, augment=False):
        self.base = base
        self.augment = augment
        self.mean = torch.tensor(
            [0.485, 0.456, 0.406]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)

=== PAGE 261 ===
        self.std = torch.tensor(
            [0.229, 0.224, 0.225]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)
        size = noise_config["gaussian_kernel"]
        sigma = noise_config["gaussian_sigma"]
        coordinates = torch.arange(size).float() - size // 2
        kernel1d = torch.exp(-coordinates.square() / (2 * sigma**2))
        kernel1d /= kernel1d.sum()
        kernel2d = kernel1d[:, None] * kernel1d[None, :]
        self.kernel = kernel2d[None, None].repeat(3, 1, 1, 1)
        self.padding = size // 2
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.base)
    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image, mask = self.base[index]
        if self.augment:
            if torch.rand(()).item() < 0.5:
                image, mask = image.flip(-1), mask.flip(-1)
            if torch.rand(()).item() < 0.5:
                image, mask = image.flip(-2), mask.flip(-2)
        padded = F.pad(
            image.unsqueeze(0),
            [self.padding] * 4,
            mode="reflect",
        )
        smooth = F.conv2d(
            padded, self.kernel, groups=3
        ).squeeze(0)
        residual = image - smooth
        rgb_normalized = (image - self.mean) / self.std
        residual_scaled = residual / self.std
        inputs = torch.cat(
            [rgb_normalized, residual_scaled], dim=0
        )
        return inputs.contiguous(), mask.float().contiguous()
torch.manual_seed(noise_config["seed"])
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(noise_config["seed"])
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
noise_train_generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(
    noise_config["seed"]
)
noise_val_generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(
    noise_config["seed"] + 1
)
noise_train_loader = DataLoader(
    RGBNoiseDataset(train_dataset, augment=True),
    batch_size=noise_config["batch_size"],
    shuffle=True,
    num_workers=0,
    pin_memory=True,
    generator=noise_train_generator,
)
noise_val_loader = DataLoader(
    RGBNoiseDataset(val_dataset, augment=False),
    batch_size=noise_config["batch_size"],
    shuffle=False,
    num_workers=0,
    pin_memory=True,
    generator=noise_val_generator,
)

=== PAGE 262 ===
print("Train batches:", len(noise_train_loader))

Train batches: 1096
Val batches: 221
import torch.nn as nn
noise_last_path = noise_dir / "last.pt"
if not noise_last_path.exists() and list(noise_dir.glob("*.pt")):
    raise RuntimeError("Có checkpoint khác nhưng thiếu last.pt.")
# Tạo model 3 kênh trước để kiểm soát việc mở rộng conv đầu.
noise_model = smp.Unet(
    encoder_name="resnet34",
    encoder_weights=None if noise_last_path.exists() else "imagenet",
    in_channels=3,
    classes=1,
    activation=None,
)
old_conv = noise_model.encoder.conv1
new_conv = nn.Conv2d(
    in_channels=6,
    out_channels=old_conv.out_channels,
    kernel_size=old_conv.kernel_size,
    stride=old_conv.stride,
    padding=old_conv.padding,
    dilation=old_conv.dilation,
    groups=old_conv.groups,
    bias=old_conv.bias is not None,
    padding_mode=old_conv.padding_mode,
)
with torch.no_grad():
    new_conv.weight.zero_()
    new_conv.weight[:, :3].copy_(old_conv.weight)
    if old_conv.bias is not None:
        new_conv.bias.copy_(old_conv.bias)
noise_model.encoder.conv1 = new_conv
# Cập nhật metadata encoder cho đầu vào 6 kênh.
noise_model.encoder._in_channels = 6
channels = list(noise_model.encoder._out_channels)
channels[0] = 6
noise_model.encoder._out_channels = tuple(channels)
noise_model = noise_model.to(noise_device)
noise_optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
    noise_model.parameters(),
    lr=noise_config["learning_rate"],
    weight_decay=noise_config["weight_decay"],
)
noise_scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda")
noise_pos_weight = torch.tensor(
    [noise_config["pos_weight"]], device=noise_device
)
def noise_loss_fn(logits, masks):
    logits, masks = logits.float(), masks.float()
    bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        logits, masks, pos_weight=noise_pos_weight
    )
    probs = logits.sigmoid()
    intersection = (probs * masks).sum()
    dice_loss = 1 - (
        (2 * intersection + 1)
        / (probs.sum() + masks.sum() + 1)
    )
    return bce + dice_loss
noise_model.eval()

    x, y = next(iter(noise_val_loader))
    assert x.shape[1:] == (6, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(x).all()
    assert ((y == 0) | (y == 1)).all()
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
        logits = noise_model(x.to(noise_device))
        loss = noise_loss_fn(logits, y.to(noise_device))
    assert logits.shape == y.shape
    assert torch.isfinite(loss)
print("Input:", tuple(x.shape))
print("Output:", tuple(logits.shape))
print("Residual scaled min/max:", x[:, 3:].min().item(), x[:, 3:].max().item())
print("Loss:", round(loss.item(), 4))
print("Parameters:", sum(p.numel() for p in noise_model.parameters()))

from tqdm.auto import tqdm
def noise_run_epoch(training):
    loader = noise_train_loader if training else noise_val_loader
    noise_model.train(training)
    thresholds = [0.5] if training else noise_config["thresholds"]
    stats = {
        t: dict(tp=0, fp=0, fn=0, dice_sum=0.0,
                n_tampered=0, auth_fp=0, auth_pixels=0)
        for t in thresholds
    }
    total_loss = 0.0
    count = 0
    max_prob = 0.0
    context = torch.enable_grad() if training else torch.inference_mode()
    with context:
        for images, masks in tqdm(
            loader,
            desc="Train" if training else "Validation",
            leave=False,
        ):
            images = images.to(noise_device, non_blocking=True)
            masks = masks.to(noise_device, non_blocking=True)
            if training:
                noise_optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                logits = noise_model(images)
                loss = noise_loss_fn(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Loss NaN/Inf.")
            if training:
                noise_scaler.scale(loss).backward()
                noise_scaler.step(noise_optimizer)
                noise_scaler.update()
            with torch.no_grad():
                probs = logits.float().sigmoid()
                assert torch.isfinite(probs).all()
                target = masks >= 0.5

=== PAGE 264 ===
                max_prob = max(max_prob, probs.max().item())
                if not training:
                    records = val_dataset.records[count:count + len(images)]
                    tampered = torch.tensor(
                        [r["label"] == "tampered" for r in records],
                        dtype=torch.bool,
                        device=noise_device,
                    )
                    assert torch.equal(
                        target.flatten(1).any(1), tampered
                    )
                for threshold, s in stats.items():
                    pred = probs >= threshold
                    dims = (1, 2, 3)
                    tp = (pred & target).sum(dims)
                    fp = (pred & ~target).sum(dims)
                    fn = (~pred & target).sum(dims)
                    s["tp"] += tp.sum().item()
                    s["fp"] += fp.sum().item()
                    s["fn"] += fn.sum().item()
                    if not training:
                        dice = 2 * tp.float() / (
                            2 * tp + fp + fn
                        ).clamp_min(1)
                        s["dice_sum"] += dice[tampered].sum().item()
                        s["n_tampered"] += tampered.sum().item()
                        authentic_pred = pred[~tampered]
                        s["auth_fp"] += authentic_pred.sum().item()
                        s["auth_pixels"] += authentic_pred.numel()
            total_loss += loss.item() * len(images)
            count += len(images)
    assert count == len(loader.dataset)
    rows = []
    for threshold, s in stats.items():
        tp, fp, fn = s["tp"], s["fp"], s["fn"]
        row = {
            "threshold": threshold,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "micro_iou": tp / max(tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "pixel_precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "pixel_recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "max_probability": max_prob,
        }
        if not training:
            row.update({
                "mean_tampered_dice": (
                    s["dice_sum"] / max(s["n_tampered"], 1)
                ),
                "authentic_fp_pixel_pct": (
                    100 * s["auth_fp"] / max(s["auth_pixels"], 1)
                ),
            })
        rows.append(row)
    return total_loss / count, rows
def noise_save(state, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    torch.save(state, temporary)
    temporary.replace(path)
def noise_csv(rows, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(temporary, index=False)
    temporary.replace(path)

def build_comparison_model(channels):
    model = smp.Unet(
        encoder_name="resnet34",
        encoder_weights=None,
        in_channels=3,
        classes=1,
        activation=None,
    )
    if channels == 6:
        old = model.encoder.conv1
        model.encoder.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(
            in_channels=6,
            out_channels=old.out_channels,
            kernel_size=old.kernel_size,
            stride=old.stride,
            padding=old.padding,
            dilation=old.dilation,
            groups=old.groups,
            bias=old.bias is not None,
            padding_mode=old.padding_mode,
        )
        model.encoder._in_channels = 6
        encoder_channels = list(model.encoder._out_channels)
        encoder_channels[0] = 6
        model.encoder._out_channels = tuple(encoder_channels)
    return model

def freq_split_signature(dataset):
    fields = [
        "image_hash", "label", "source_group",
        "mask_verified", "mask_source",
        "mask_threshold", "threshold_approved",
    ]
    records = [
        {
            **{k: str(row.get(k, "")) for k in fields},
            "mask_name": (

Dataset RGB + DCT
class RGBFrequencyDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, base, augment=False):
        self.base = base
        self.augment = augment

=== PAGE 275 ===
        self.mean = torch.tensor(
            [0.485, 0.456, 0.406]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)
        self.std = torch.tensor(
            [0.229, 0.224, 0.225]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)
        self.gray_weights = torch.tensor(
            freq_config["grayscale_weights"]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)
        self.block_size = freq_config["block_size"]
        n = self.block_size
        # Ma trận DCT-II trực giao: C[k, x].
        k = torch.arange(n).float().view(n, 1)
        x = torch.arange(n).float().view(1, n)
        matrix = torch.cos(math.pi * (x + 0.5) * k / n)
        matrix *= math.sqrt(2.0 / n)
        matrix[0] /= math.sqrt(2.0)
        self.dct_matrix = matrix
        u = torch.arange(n).view(n, 1)
        v = torch.arange(n).view(1, n)
        frequency_index = u + v
        self.band_masks = [
            (frequency_index >= low) & (frequency_index <= high)
            for low, high in freq_config["bands_u_plus_v"]
        ]
        # 64 là chặn trên năng lượng toàn block 8x8
        # khi grayscale nằm trong [0,1].
        self.log_scale = math.log1p(n * n)
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.base)
    def frequency_maps(self, image):
        gray = (image * self.gray_weights).sum(dim=0)
        h, w = gray.shape
        n = self.block_size
        assert h % n == 0 and w % n == 0
        # [block_rows, block_cols, 8, 8]
        blocks = gray.unfold(0, n, n).unfold(1, n, n)
        # DCT2(block) = C @ block @ C.T
        coefficients = (
            self.dct_matrix
            @ blocks
            @ self.dct_matrix.T
        )
        energy = coefficients.square()
        maps = torch.stack([
            energy[..., band].mean(dim=-1)
            for band in self.band_masks
        ], dim=0)
        maps = torch.log1p(maps) / self.log_scale
        return F.interpolate(
            maps.unsqueeze(0),
            size=(h, w),
            mode="nearest",
        ).squeeze(0)
    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image, mask = self.base[index]
        if self.augment:
            if torch.rand(()).item() < 0.5:

=== PAGE 276 ===
                image, mask = image.flip(-1), mask.flip(-1)
            if torch.rand(()).item() < 0.5:
                image, mask = image.flip(-2), mask.flip(-2)
        frequency = self.frequency_maps(image)
        rgb = (image - self.mean) / self.std
        inputs = torch.cat([rgb, frequency], dim=0)
        return inputs.contiguous(), mask.float().contiguous()

with torch.no_grad():

    if old_conv.bias is not None:
        new_conv.bias.copy_(old_conv.bias)

    freq_model.parameters(),
    lr=freq_config["learning_rate"],
    weight_decay=freq_config["weight_decay"],
)
freq_scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda")
freq_pos_weight = torch.tensor(
    [freq_config["pos_weight"]], device=freq_device
)
def freq_loss_fn(logits, masks):
    logits, masks = logits.float(), masks.float()
    bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        logits, masks, pos_weight=freq_pos_weight
    )
    probs = logits.sigmoid()
    intersection = (probs * masks).sum()
    dice_loss = 1 - (
        (2 * intersection + 1)
        / (probs.sum() + masks.sum() + 1)
    )
    return bce + dice_loss

Hàm train và validation
def freq_run_epoch(training):
    loader = freq_train_loader if training else freq_val_loader
    freq_model.train(training)
    thresholds = [0.5] if training else freq_config["thresholds"]
    stats = {
        t: dict(
            tp=0, fp=0, fn=0,
            dice_sum=0.0, n_tampered=0,
            auth_fp=0, auth_pixels=0,
        )
        for t in thresholds
    }
    total_loss = 0.0
    count = 0
    max_prob = 0.0
    context = (
        torch.enable_grad()
        if training else torch.inference_mode()
    )
    with context:
        for images, masks in tqdm(
            loader,
            desc="Train" if training else "Validation",
            leave=False,
        ):
            images = images.to(freq_device, non_blocking=True)
            masks = masks.to(freq_device, non_blocking=True)
            if training:
                freq_optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                logits = freq_model(images)
                loss = freq_loss_fn(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Loss NaN/Inf.")
            if training:
                freq_scaler.scale(loss).backward()
                freq_scaler.step(freq_optimizer)
                freq_scaler.update()

=== PAGE 279 ===
            with torch.no_grad():
                probs = logits.float().sigmoid()
                assert torch.isfinite(probs).all()
                target = masks >= 0.5
                max_prob = max(max_prob, probs.max().item())
                if not training:
                    records = val_dataset.records[
                        count:count + len(images)
                    ]
                    tampered = torch.tensor(
                        [r["label"] == "tampered" for r in records],
                        dtype=torch.bool,
                        device=freq_device,
                    )
                    assert torch.equal(
                        target.flatten(1).any(1), tampered
                    )
                for threshold, s in stats.items():
                    pred = probs >= threshold
                    dims = (1, 2, 3)
                    tp = (pred & target).sum(dims)
                    fp = (pred & ~target).sum(dims)
                    fn = (~pred & target).sum(dims)
                    s["tp"] += tp.sum().item()
                    s["fp"] += fp.sum().item()
                    s["fn"] += fn.sum().item()
                    if not training:
                        dice = 2 * tp.float() / (
                            2 * tp + fp + fn
                        ).clamp_min(1)
                        s["dice_sum"] += dice[tampered].sum().item()
                        s["n_tampered"] += tampered.sum().item()
                        authentic_pred = pred[~tampered]
                        s["auth_fp"] += authentic_pred.sum().item()
                        s["auth_pixels"] += authentic_pred.numel()
            total_loss += loss.item() * len(images)
            count += len(images)
    assert count == len(loader.dataset)
    rows = []
    for threshold, s in stats.items():
        tp, fp, fn = s["tp"], s["fp"], s["fn"]
        row = {
            "threshold": threshold,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "micro_iou": tp / max(tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "pixel_precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "pixel_recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "max_probability": max_prob,
        }
        if not training:
            row.update({
                "mean_tampered_dice": (
                    s["dice_sum"] / max(s["n_tampered"], 1)
                ),
                "authentic_fp_pixel_pct": (
                    100 * s["auth_fp"] / max(s["auth_pixels"], 1)
                ),
            })
        rows.append(row)
    return total_loss / count, rows

def freq_save(state, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    torch.save(state, temporary)
    temporary.replace(path)
def freq_save_csv(rows, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(temporary, index=False)
    temporary.replace(path)

def freq_state(epoch):
    return {
        "epoch": epoch,
        "config": freq_config,
        "model": freq_model.state_dict(),
        "optimizer": freq_optimizer.state_dict(),
        "scaler": freq_scaler.state_dict(),
        "history": freq_history,
        "threshold_history": freq_threshold_history,
        "best_dice": freq_best_dice,
        "best_dice_loss": freq_best_dice_loss,
        "best_loss": freq_best_loss,
        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
        "cuda_rng": torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all(),
        "train_loader_rng": freq_train_generator.get_state(),
        "val_loader_rng": freq_val_generator.get_state(),

    freq_threshold_history = saved["threshold_history"]

    freq_best_dice_loss = saved["best_dice_loss"]

def m_split_signature(dataset):
    fields = [
        "image_hash", "label", "source_group",
        "mask_verified", "mask_source",
        "mask_threshold", "threshold_approved",
    ]
    rows = [
        {
            **{k: str(record.get(k, "")) for k in fields},
            "mask_name": (
                Path(record["mask_path"]).name
                if record["mask_path"] is not None else ""
            ),
        }
        for record in dataset.records
    ]
    text = json.dumps(rows, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

def m_seed(*parts):
    text = "|".join(str(part) for part in parts)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).digest()
    return int.from_bytes(digest[:8], "little") % (2**63 - 1)
class MultiInputDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, base, mode, seed, augment=False):
        assert mode in M_MODES
        self.base = base

=== PAGE 286 ===
        self.mode = mode
        self.seed = seed
        self.augment = augment
        self.epoch = 0
        self.mean = torch.tensor(
            [0.485, 0.456, 0.406]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)
        self.std = torch.tensor(
            [0.229, 0.224, 0.225]
        ).view(3, 1, 1)
        if mode == "noise":
            coordinate = torch.arange(5).float() - 2
            kernel1d = torch.exp(-coordinate.square() / 2)
            kernel1d /= kernel1d.sum()
            kernel2d = kernel1d[:, None] * kernel1d[None, :]
            self.gaussian_kernel = kernel2d[None, None].repeat(
                3, 1, 1, 1
            )
        if mode == "frequency":
            self.gray_weights = torch.tensor(
                [0.299, 0.587, 0.114]
            ).view(3, 1, 1)
            n = 8
            k = torch.arange(n).float().view(n, 1)
            x = torch.arange(n).float().view(1, n)
            matrix = torch.cos(math.pi * (x + 0.5) * k / n)
            matrix *= math.sqrt(2 / n)
            matrix[0] /= math.sqrt(2)
            self.dct_matrix = matrix
            uv = (
                torch.arange(n).view(n, 1)
                + torch.arange(n).view(1, n)
            )
            self.band_masks = [
                (uv >= low) & (uv <= high)
                for low, high in [(1, 3), (4, 7), (8, 14)]
            ]
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.base)
    def set_epoch(self, epoch):
        self.epoch = epoch
    def flip_flags(self, index):
        generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(
            m_seed("augmentation", self.seed, self.epoch, index)
        )
        flags = torch.rand(2, generator=generator) < 0.5
        return bool(flags[0]), bool(flags[1])
    def extra_features(self, image):
        if self.mode == "noise":
            padded = F.pad(
                image.unsqueeze(0), [2, 2, 2, 2], mode="reflect"
            )
            smooth = F.conv2d(
                padded, self.gaussian_kernel, groups=3
            ).squeeze(0)
            return (image - smooth) / self.std
        if self.mode == "frequency":
            gray = (image * self.gray_weights).sum(0)
            h, w = gray.shape
            assert h % 8 == 0 and w % 8 == 0
            blocks = gray.unfold(0, 8, 8).unfold(1, 8, 8)
            coefficients = (
                self.dct_matrix @ blocks @ self.dct_matrix.T
            )
            energy = coefficients.square()

=== PAGE 287 ===
            maps = torch.stack([
                energy[..., band].mean(-1)
                for band in self.band_masks
            ])
            maps = torch.log1p(maps) / math.log(65)
            return F.interpolate(
                maps.unsqueeze(0),
                size=(h, w),
                mode="nearest",
            ).squeeze(0)
        raise ValueError("RGB không có extra features.")
    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image, mask = self.base[index]
        if self.augment:
            horizontal, vertical = self.flip_flags(index)
            if horizontal:
                image, mask = image.flip(-1), mask.flip(-1)
            if vertical:
                image, mask = image.flip(-2), mask.flip(-2)
        rgb = (image - self.mean) / self.std
        if self.mode == "rgb":
            inputs = rgb
        else:
            inputs = torch.cat(
                [rgb, self.extra_features(image)], dim=0
            )
        return inputs.contiguous(), mask.float().contiguous()
# Kiểm tra cùng seed/epoch/index -> cùng RGB và mask sau augmentation.
check_datasets = [
    MultiInputDataset(train_dataset, mode, seed=7, augment=True)
    for mode in M_MODES
]
for dataset in check_datasets:
    dataset.set_epoch(3)
for index in [0, len(train_dataset) // 2, len(train_dataset) - 1]:
    reference_x, reference_y = check_datasets[0][index]
    for dataset in check_datasets[1:]:
        x, y = dataset[index]
        assert torch.equal(x[:3], reference_x)
        assert torch.equal(y, reference_y)
        assert torch.isfinite(x).all()
del check_datasets
print("PASS: cùng augmentation cho RGB, Noise và Frequency.")
def m_build_model(mode, seed, pretrained):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)
    model = smp.Unet(
        encoder_name="resnet34",
        encoder_weights="imagenet" if pretrained else None,

=== PAGE 288 ===
        in_channels=3,
        classes=1,
        activation=None,
    )
    if mode != "rgb":
        old = model.encoder.conv1
        # Khôi phục CPU RNG sau khi tạo conv bổ sung.
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
            new = nn.Conv2d(
                in_channels=6,
                out_channels=old.out_channels,
                kernel_size=old.kernel_size,
                stride=old.stride,
                padding=old.padding,
                dilation=old.dilation,
                groups=old.groups,
                bias=old.bias is not None,
                padding_mode=old.padding_mode,
            )
        with torch.no_grad():
            new.weight.zero_()
            new.weight[:, :3].copy_(old.weight)
            if old.bias is not None:
                new.bias.copy_(old.bias)
        model.encoder.conv1 = new
        model.encoder._in_channels = 6
        channels = list(model.encoder._out_channels)
        channels[0] = 6
        model.encoder._out_channels = tuple(channels)
    return model.to(M_DEVICE)
def m_loss(logits, masks):
    logits, masks = logits.float(), masks.float()
    bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        logits,
        masks,
        pos_weight=logits.new_tensor([3.0]),
    )
    probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
    intersection = (probabilities * masks).sum()
    dice_loss = 1 - (
        (2 * intersection + 1)
        / (probabilities.sum() + masks.sum() + 1)
    )
    return bce + dice_loss
def m_epoch(model, loader, training, optimizer=None, scaler=None):
    model.train(training)
    thresholds = [0.5] if training else M_THRESHOLDS
    stats = {
        threshold: {
            "tp": 0, "fp": 0, "fn": 0,
            "dice_sum": 0.0, "n_tampered": 0,
            "auth_fp": 0, "auth_pixels": 0,
        }
        for threshold in thresholds
    }
    total_loss = 0.0
    count = 0
    max_probability = 0.0
    context = (
        torch.enable_grad()
        if training else torch.inference_mode()
    )

=== PAGE 289 ===
    with context:
        for images, masks in tqdm(
            loader,
            desc="Train" if training else "Validation",
            leave=False,
        ):
            images = images.to(M_DEVICE, non_blocking=True)
            masks = masks.to(M_DEVICE, non_blocking=True)
            if training:
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                logits = model(images)
                loss = m_loss(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Loss NaN/Inf. Epoch chưa được lưu.")
            if training:
                scaler.scale(loss).backward()
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()
            with torch.no_grad():
                probs = logits.float().sigmoid()
                assert torch.isfinite(probs).all()
                target = masks >= 0.5
                max_probability = max(
                    max_probability, probs.max().item()
                )
                if not training:
                    records = val_dataset.records[
                        count:count + len(images)
                    ]
                    tampered = torch.tensor(
                        [r["label"] == "tampered" for r in records],
                        dtype=torch.bool,
                        device=M_DEVICE,
                    )
                    assert torch.equal(
                        target.flatten(1).any(1), tampered
                    )
                for threshold, s in stats.items():
                    pred = probs >= threshold
                    dimensions = (1, 2, 3)
                    tp = (pred & target).sum(dimensions)
                    fp = (pred & ~target).sum(dimensions)
                    fn = (~pred & target).sum(dimensions)
                    s["tp"] += tp.sum().item()
                    s["fp"] += fp.sum().item()
                    s["fn"] += fn.sum().item()
                    if not training:
                        dice = 2 * tp.float() / (
                            2 * tp + fp + fn
                        ).clamp_min(1)
                        s["dice_sum"] += dice[tampered].sum().item()
                        s["n_tampered"] += tampered.sum().item()
                        authentic_pred = pred[~tampered]
                        s["auth_fp"] += authentic_pred.sum().item()
                        s["auth_pixels"] += authentic_pred.numel()
            total_loss += loss.item() * len(images)
            count += len(images)
    assert count == len(loader.dataset)
    rows = []

=== PAGE 290 ===
    for threshold, s in stats.items():
        tp, fp, fn = s["tp"], s["fp"], s["fn"]
        row = {
            "threshold": threshold,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "micro_iou": tp / max(tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "pixel_precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "pixel_recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "max_probability": max_probability,
        }
        if not training:
            row.update({
                "mean_tampered_dice": (
                    s["dice_sum"] / max(s["n_tampered"], 1)
                ),
                "authentic_fp_pixel_pct": (
                    100 * s["auth_fp"] / max(s["auth_pixels"], 1)
                ),
            })
        rows.append(row)
    return total_loss / count, rows
def m_save(state, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    torch.save(state, temporary)
    temporary.replace(path)
def m_csv(rows, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(temporary, index=False)
    temporary.replace(path)
def m_cpu_weights(model):
    return {
        key: value.detach().cpu().clone()
        for key, value in model.state_dict().items()
    }

def m_split_signature(dataset):
    fields = [
        "image_hash",
        "label",
        "source_group",
        "mask_verified",
        "mask_source",
        "mask_threshold",
        "threshold_approved",
    ]
    rows = [
        {
            **{
                key: str(record.get(key, ""))
                for key in fields
            },
            "mask_name": (
                Path(record["mask_path"]).name
                if record["mask_path"] is not None
                else ""
            ),
        }

=== PAGE 292 ===
        for record in dataset.records
    ]
    text = json.dumps(
        rows, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False
    )
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

def m_seed(*parts):
    text = "|".join(str(part) for part in parts)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).digest()
    return (
        int.from_bytes(digest[:8], "little")
        % (2**63 - 1)
