M4
def m_build_model(mode, seed, pretrained):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)
    model = smp.Unet(
        encoder_name="resnet34",
        encoder_weights=(
            "imagenet" if pretrained else None
        ),
        in_channels=3,
        classes=1,
        activation=None,
    )
    if mode != "rgb":
        old = model.encoder.conv1
        # Không làm dịch CPU RNG khi tạo conv bổ sung.
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
            new = nn.Conv2d(
                in_channels=6,
                out_channels=old.out_channels,
                kernel_size=old.kernel_size,
                stride=old.stride,
                padding=old.padding,
                dilation=old.dilation,
                groups=old.groups,
                bias=old.bias is not None,
                padding_mode=old.padding_mode,
            )
        with torch.no_grad():
            new.weight.zero_()
            new.weight[:, :3].copy_(old.weight)
            if old.bias is not None:
                new.bias.copy_(old.bias)
        model.encoder.conv1 = new
        model.encoder._in_channels = 6
        channels = list(model.encoder._out_channels)
        channels[0] = 6
        model.encoder._out_channels = tuple(channels)
    return model.to(M_DEVICE)
def m_loss(logits, masks):
    logits = logits.float()
    masks = masks.float()

=== PAGE 297 ===
    bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        logits,
        masks,
        pos_weight=logits.new_tensor([3.0]),
    )
    probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
    intersection = (probabilities * masks).sum()
    dice_loss = 1 - (
        (2 * intersection + 1)
        / (probabilities.sum() + masks.sum() + 1)
    )
    return bce + dice_loss
def m_epoch(
    model,
    loader,
    training,
    optimizer=None,
    scaler=None,
):
    model.train(training)
    thresholds = (
        [0.5] if training else M_THRESHOLDS
    )
    stats = {
        threshold: {
            "tp": 0,
            "fp": 0,
            "fn": 0,
            "dice_sum": 0.0,
            "n_tampered": 0,
            "auth_fp": 0,
            "auth_pixels": 0,
        }
        for threshold in thresholds
    }
    total_loss = 0.0
    count = 0
    max_probability = 0.0
    context = (
        torch.enable_grad()
        if training
        else torch.inference_mode()
    )
    with context:
        for images, masks in tqdm(
            loader,
            desc="Train" if training else "Validation",
            leave=False,
        ):
            images = images.to(
                M_DEVICE, non_blocking=True
            )
            masks = masks.to(
                M_DEVICE, non_blocking=True
            )
            if training:
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            with torch.autocast(
                "cuda", dtype=torch.float16
            ):
                logits = model(images)
                loss = m_loss(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():

=== PAGE 298 ===
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Loss NaN/Inf. Epoch chưa được lưu."
                )
            if training:
                scaler.scale(loss).backward()
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()
            with torch.no_grad():
                probs = logits.float().sigmoid()
                assert torch.isfinite(probs).all()
                target = masks >= 0.5
                max_probability = max(
                    max_probability,
                    probs.max().item(),
                )
                if not training:
                    records = val_dataset.records[
                        count:count + len(images)
                    ]
                    tampered = torch.tensor(
                        [
                            record["label"] == "tampered"
                            for record in records
                        ],
                        dtype=torch.bool,
                        device=M_DEVICE,
                    )
                    assert torch.equal(
                        target.flatten(1).any(1),
                        tampered,
                    )
                for threshold, s in stats.items():
                    pred = probs >= threshold
                    dimensions = (1, 2, 3)
                    tp = (pred & target).sum(dimensions)
                    fp = (pred & ~target).sum(dimensions)
                    fn = (~pred & target).sum(dimensions)
                    s["tp"] += tp.sum().item()
                    s["fp"] += fp.sum().item()
                    s["fn"] += fn.sum().item()
                    if not training:
                        dice = (
                            2 * tp.float()
                            / (2 * tp + fp + fn).clamp_min(1)
                        )
                        s["dice_sum"] += (
                            dice[tampered].sum().item()
                        )
                        s["n_tampered"] += (
                            tampered.sum().item()
                        )
                        authentic_pred = pred[~tampered]
                        s["auth_fp"] += (
                            authentic_pred.sum().item()
                        )
                        s["auth_pixels"] += (
                            authentic_pred.numel()
                        )
            total_loss += loss.item() * len(images)
            count += len(images)
    assert count == len(loader.dataset)

=== PAGE 299 ===
    rows = []
    for threshold, s in stats.items():
        tp = s["tp"]
        fp = s["fp"]
        fn = s["fn"]
        row = {
            "threshold": threshold,
            "micro_dice": (
                2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1)
            ),
            "micro_iou": tp / max(tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "pixel_precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "pixel_recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "max_probability": max_probability,
        }
        if not training:
            row.update({
                "mean_tampered_dice": (
                    s["dice_sum"]
                    / max(s["n_tampered"], 1)
                ),
                "authentic_fp_pixel_pct": (
                    100 * s["auth_fp"]
                    / max(s["auth_pixels"], 1)
                ),
            })
        rows.append(row)
    return total_loss / count, rows
def m_save(state, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    torch.save(state, temporary)
    temporary.replace(path)
def m_csv(rows, path):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(temporary, index=False)
    temporary.replace(path)
def m_cpu_weights(model):
    return {
        key: value.detach().cpu().clone()
        for key, value in model.state_dict().items()
    }

def m_export_reports(saved, directory):
    m_csv(
        saved["history"],
        directory / "history.csv",
    )
    m_csv(
        saved["threshold_history"],
        directory / "validation_thresholds.csv",
    )
    if saved["best_model"] is not None:
        m_save(
            {
                "config": saved["config"],
                "epoch": saved["best_epoch"],
                "model": saved["best_model"],

=== PAGE 300 ===
                "selection_metrics": saved["best_metrics"],
            },
            directory / "best_dice.pt",
        )
def m_run(mode, seed):
    config = m_config(mode, seed)
    directory = M_ROOT / f"{mode}_seed{seed}"
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    config_path = directory / "config.json"
    last_path = directory / "last.pt"
    if config_path.exists():
        assert json.loads(config_path.read_text()) == config, (
            f"Config không khớp: {directory}"
        )
    else:
        assert not list(directory.glob("*.pt")), (
            "Có checkpoint nhưng thiếu config.json."
        )
        config_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(config, indent=2),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
    saved = None
    if last_path.exists():
        saved = torch.load(
            last_path,
            map_location="cpu",
            weights_only=True,
        )
        assert saved["config"] == config
        # Khôi phục CSV/best checkpoint nếu lần trước bị
        # ngắt sau khi lưu last.pt nhưng trước khi xuất chúng.
        m_export_reports(saved, directory)
        if saved["epoch"] >= config["epochs"]:
            print(
                f"SKIP {mode}, seed {seed}: đã hoàn thành.",
                flush=True,
            )
            del saved
            gc.collect()
            return
    elif list(directory.glob("*.pt")):
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Có checkpoint nhưng thiếu last.pt: {directory}"
        )
    model = m_build_model(
        mode,
        seed,
        pretrained=(saved is None),
    )
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(),
        lr=config["learning_rate"],
        weight_decay=config["weight_decay"],
    )
    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda")
    train_data = MultiInputDataset(
        train_dataset,
        mode,
        seed,
        augment=True,
    )
    val_data = MultiInputDataset(
        val_dataset,

=== PAGE 301 ===
        mode,
        seed,
        augment=False,
    )
    shuffle_generator = torch.Generator()
    validation_generator = torch.Generator()
    train_loader_local = DataLoader(
        train_data,
        batch_size=config["batch_size"],
        shuffle=True,
        num_workers=0,
        pin_memory=True,
        generator=shuffle_generator,
    )
    val_loader_local = DataLoader(
        val_data,
        batch_size=config["batch_size"],
        shuffle=False,
        num_workers=0,
        pin_memory=True,
        generator=validation_generator,
    )
    history = []
    threshold_history = []
    best_model = None
    best_epoch = None
    best_metrics = None
    best_dice = -1.0
    best_loss_at_dice = float("inf")
    start_epoch = 1
    def capture(epoch):
        return {
            "config": config,
            "epoch": epoch,
            "model": model.state_dict(),
            "optimizer": optimizer.state_dict(),
            "scaler": scaler.state_dict(),
            "history": history,
            "threshold_history": threshold_history,
            "best_model": best_model,
            "best_epoch": best_epoch,
            "best_metrics": best_metrics,
            "best_dice": best_dice,
            "best_loss_at_dice": best_loss_at_dice,
            "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
            "cuda_rng": torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all(),
        }
    try:
        if saved is not None:
            model.load_state_dict(
                saved["model"], strict=True
            )
            optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer"])
            scaler.load_state_dict(saved["scaler"])
            history = saved["history"]
            threshold_history = saved["threshold_history"]
            best_model = saved["best_model"]
            best_epoch = saved["best_epoch"]
            best_metrics = saved["best_metrics"]
            best_dice = saved["best_dice"]
            best_loss_at_dice = saved["best_loss_at_dice"]
            start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
            torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
            torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all(saved["cuda_rng"])
            del saved
            saved = None

=== PAGE 302 ===
            print(
                f"\nRESUME {mode} | seed {seed} | "
                f"epoch {start_epoch}",
                flush=True,
            )
        else:
            # Kiểm tra một sample, không dùng train loader.
            model.eval()
            with torch.inference_mode():
                check_x, check_y = val_data[0]
                assert torch.isfinite(check_x).all()
                assert (
                    (check_y == 0) | (check_y == 1)
                ).all()
                with torch.autocast(
                    "cuda", dtype=torch.float16
                ):
                    check_logits = model(
                        check_x.unsqueeze(0).to(M_DEVICE)
                    )
                    check_loss = m_loss(
                        check_logits,
                        check_y.unsqueeze(0).to(M_DEVICE),
                    )
                assert check_logits.shape == (
                    1, 1, 256, 256
                )
                assert torch.isfinite(check_loss)
            del check_x, check_y, check_logits, check_loss
            # Lưu epoch 0 để có thể resume nếu epoch 1 bị ngắt.
            m_save(capture(0), last_path)
            print(
                f"\nNEW {mode} | seed {seed}",
                flush=True,
            )
        for epoch in range(
            start_epoch, config["epochs"] + 1
        ):
            train_data.set_epoch(epoch)
            shuffle_generator.manual_seed(
                m_seed("shuffle", seed, epoch)
            )
            validation_generator.manual_seed(
                m_seed("validation_loader", seed, epoch)
            )
            train_loss, train_rows = m_epoch(
                model,
                train_loader_local,
                training=True,
                optimizer=optimizer,
                scaler=scaler,
            )
            val_loss, val_rows = m_epoch(
                model,
                val_loader_local,
                training=False,
            )
            val05 = next(
                row for row in val_rows
                if row["threshold"] == 0.5
            )
            score = val05["micro_dice"]

=== PAGE 303 ===
            improved = (
                score > best_dice
                or (
                    score == best_dice
                    and val_loss < best_loss_at_dice
                )
            )
            if improved:
                best_dice = score
                best_loss_at_dice = val_loss
                best_epoch = epoch
                best_metrics = {
                    "val_loss": val_loss,
                    **val05,
                }
                best_model = m_cpu_weights(model)
            history.append({
                "epoch": epoch,
                "train_loss": train_loss,
                "train_online_dice_05": (
                    train_rows[0]["micro_dice"]
                ),
                "val_loss": val_loss,
                **{
                    f"val_{key}": value
                    for key, value in val05.items()
                    if key != "threshold"
                },
            })
            threshold_history.extend(
                {"epoch": epoch, **row}
                for row in val_rows
            )
            state = capture(epoch)
            # Lưu checkpoint trước khi in thông báo epoch.
            m_save(state, last_path)
            m_export_reports(state, directory)
            del state
            print(
                f"{mode} | seed {seed} | "
                f"Epoch {epoch:02d}/10 | "
                f"Train loss {train_loss:.4f} | "
                f"Val loss {val_loss:.4f} | "
                f"Dice@0.5 {score:.4f} | "
                f"Mean tampered Dice "
                f"{val05['mean_tampered_dice']:.4f} | "
                f"Authentic FP pixels "
                f"{val05['authentic_fp_pixel_pct']:.2f}%",
                flush=True,
            )
        print(
            f"DONE {mode}, seed {seed}: "
            f"best epoch {best_epoch}, "
            f"Dice {best_dice:.6f}",
            flush=True,
        )
    finally:
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        model.cpu()
        del optimizer, scaler, model, best_model, saved
        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

            continue
        assert history[required_columns].notna().all().all(), (
            f"Có giá trị thiếu trong history: {directory}"
        )
        # Giống quy tắc chọn best_dice.pt:
        # micro Dice cao nhất, hòa thì loss thấp hơn.
        best = history.sort_values(
            ["val_micro_dice", "val_loss", "epoch"],
            ascending=[False, True, True],
            kind="stable",
        ).iloc[0]
        result_rows.append({
            "mode": mode,

=== PAGE 310 ===
            "seed": seed,
            "best_epoch": int(best["epoch"]),
            "micro_dice": best["val_micro_dice"],
            "micro_iou": best["val_micro_iou"],
            "mean_tampered_dice": (
                best["val_mean_tampered_dice"]
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pixel_pct": (
                best["val_authentic_fp_pixel_pct"]
            ),
        })
status_table = pd.DataFrame(status_rows)
print("A. TRẠNG THÁI 9 LƯỢT")
display(status_table)
results = pd.DataFrame(result_rows)
if results.empty:
    print("Chưa có lượt hoàn thành để tổng hợp.")
else:
    results = results.sort_values(
        ["mode", "seed"]
    ).reset_index(drop=True)
    print("\nB. KẾT QUẢ TỪNG SEED")
    display(results.round(6))
    metrics = [
        "micro_dice",
        "micro_iou",
        "mean_tampered_dice",
        "authentic_fp_pixel_pct",
    ]
    # Tạo bảng phẳng, dễ copy vào báo cáo.
    aggregate_rows = []
    for mode in REPORT_MODES:
        frame = results[results["mode"] == mode]
        if frame.empty:
            continue
        row = {
            "mode": mode,
            "n_seeds": len(frame),
        }
        for metric in metrics:
            row[f"{metric}_mean"] = frame[metric].mean()
            row[f"{metric}_std"] = frame[metric].std(ddof=1)
        aggregate_rows.append(row)
    aggregate = pd.DataFrame(aggregate_rows)
    print("\nC. MEAN VÀ SAMPLE STANDARD DEVIATION")
    display(aggregate.round(6))
    # Bảng hiển thị gọn mean ± std.
    formatted_rows = []
    for _, row in aggregate.iterrows():
        formatted = {
            "mode": row["mode"],
            "n_seeds": int(row["n_seeds"]),
        }
        for metric in metrics:
            mean = row[f"{metric}_mean"]
            std = row[f"{metric}_std"]
            formatted[metric] = (

=== PAGE 311 ===
                f"{mean:.6f} ± {std:.6f}"
                if pd.notna(std)
                else f"{mean:.6f} ± N/A"
            )
        formatted_rows.append(formatted)
    print("\nD. BẢNG GỌN: MEAN ± SD")
    display(pd.DataFrame(formatted_rows))
    # Chênh lệch theo cặp, cùng seed.
    pivot = results.pivot(
        index="seed",
        columns="mode",
        values="micro_dice",
    ).reindex(
        index=REPORT_SEEDS,
        columns=REPORT_MODES,
    )
    paired = pd.DataFrame(index=pivot.index)
    paired.index.name = "seed"
    paired["noise_minus_rgb"] = (
        pivot["noise"] - pivot["rgb"]
    )
    paired["frequency_minus_rgb"] = (
        pivot["frequency"] - pivot["rgb"]
    )
    paired["noise_minus_frequency"] = (
        pivot["noise"] - pivot["frequency"]
    )
    print("\nE. CHÊNH LỆCH MICRO DICE THEO CẶP SEED")
    display(paired.round(6))
    all_complete = (
        len(results) == 9
        and (status_table["status"] == "complete").all()
    )
    # Tách tên báo cáo tạm và báo cáo đủ ba seed.
    report_name = (
        "three_seed_report"
        if all_complete
        else "three_seed_report_partial"
    )
    report_dir = M_ROOT / report_name
    report_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    status_table.to_csv(
        report_dir / "status.csv", index=False
    )
    results.to_csv(
        report_dir / "per_seed_results.csv", index=False
    )
    aggregate.to_csv(
        report_dir / "mean_std.csv", index=False
    )
    paired.to_csv(
        report_dir / "paired_micro_dice.csv"
    )
    print("\nĐã lưu báo cáo:", report_dir)
    if all_complete:
        print("ĐỦ 3 SEED × 3 CẤU HÌNH.")
    else:
        print(
            "CHƯA ĐỦ 9 LƯỢT: bảng hiện tại chỉ gồm "
            "những lượt đã hoàn thành."
        )
